Given a multimodal query, reference image, and reference audio, the model predicts a structured \textit{Visual Thought Plan} (VTP) describing scene, emotion, and motion, followed by response text and native multi-codebook speech units. These units form a shared acoustic-temporal interface: they are decoded into speech and aligned online with video frames. This interface enables the response and avatar pathways to be learned from heterogeneous speech, dialogue, and avatar-video data, avoiding the need for large-scale query--text--speech--video supervision. A full-sequence Video Generator serves as the primary Teacher. For efficient incremental generation, we further distill it into a few-step block-causal \emph{Streaming Student} whose Prefix Streaming mechanism carries a clean latent across consecutive chunks to reduce cumulative late-chunk degradation. With four-step inference, the complete four-GPU pipeline achieves an end-to-end RTF of 1.293 at $400\times720$/$720\times400$, providing a practical quality--efficiency operating point.

</details>


### [239] [Tree-of-Ideas: Automated Research Ideation via Cross-Trajectory Reasoning over Scholarly Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10740)
*Xun Li,Yiying Yang,Pengtao Li,Xiao Yao,Suyu Liu,Xiaoyang Ye,Ziyu Lu,Yuan Yao,Yangning Li,Yinghui Li,Wenhao Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Tree-of-Ideas (ToI)框架，通过两阶段方法（EvoTrace重构分支化学术轨迹 + EvoAgent跨轨迹推理）生成研究想法，在6个AI主题上取得最高自动评分（6.27/10），接近人类论文参考水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究构思方法要么将论文视为非结构化上下文，要么将学术演化建模为孤立的引用链，忽视了研究轨迹之间的交互关系。作者认为，有效的研究构思需要超越对前人工作的静态理解，追踪研究问题和解决方案在文献中的演化过程。

Method: 提出Tree-of-Ideas (ToI)两阶段框架：第一阶段EvoTrace从引用关系中重构分支化学术轨迹，追踪演化的方法、已解决的问题和研究空白；第二阶段EvoAgent跨轨迹进行推理，识别收敛性问题和互补性解决方案，最终生成有根基的研究想法。

Result: 在6个AI研究主题上，ToI在10分制评分中取得6.27分，显著优于最强基线（5.36分），其中新颖性得分6.36、根基性得分7.00。此外，其得分接近人类论文参考文献水平（6.29分），验证了跨路径演化推理的价值。

Conclusion: ToI通过建模研究轨迹的分支演化与跨轨迹交互，证明了跨路径演化推理对研究构思的有效性，为自动化研究想法生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Effective research ideation requires moving beyond a static understanding of prior work to trace how research problems and solutions evolve across the literature. Existing methods either treat papers as unstructured context or model scholarly evolution as isolated citation chains, overlooking interactions among research trajectories. We propose Tree-of-Ideas (ToI), a two-stage framework. EvoTrace reconstructs branching scholarly trajectories from citations, tracking evolving methods, resolved problems, and gaps. EvoAgent then reasons across trajectories to identify convergent problems and complementary solutions, generating grounded research ideas. Across six AI research topics, ToI achieves the highest score among automatic methods (6.27 vs. 5.36 for the strongest baseline on a 10-point scale), with strong Novelty (6.36) and Groundedness (7.00). Also, its score approaches that of human-paper references (6.29), demonstrating the value of cross-path evolutionary reasoning.

</details>


### [240] [Compositional Benchmark Synthesis for Hierarchical Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10765)
*Farnaz Soleimani,Abdelghani Chibani,Yacine Amirat,Ghazaleh Khodabandelou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个四层语义层次化意图理解基准的自动生成框架，通过在原始单标签动作语料上施加转移模型和覆盖感知采样器，合成动作-活动-低层意图-高层意图的分层标注数据，同时通过设计将生成规则与评估规则解耦以避免循环监督风险。实验表明，各基线模型在组合性留出集上普遍存在0.13-0.17 macro-F1的性能差距，揭示这是基准的结构性属性而非模型缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行为理解数据集存在两极分化：大规模语料仅提供孤立的原子级标注，缺乏时序组合；而复合活动语料层次浅、领域窄且层次结构固定。亟需一个能自动生成多层语义层次标注的基准框架，同时必须解决合成数据中循环监督（即生成规则与评估规则同源导致模型可投机取巧）的有效性问题。

Method: 提出基准生成与评估框架，包含四个核心设计：(1) 从单标签动作语料出发，利用转移模型在主体一致性约束下组装片段序列，合成四层层次（动作、活动、低层意图LLI、高层意图HLI）；(2) 引入覆盖感知采样器，将主体使用基尼系数从0.566降至0.248；(3) 通过设计使序列生成规则与评估所用的一阶逻辑规则保持互斥，规避循环监督；(4) 最终生成15,002个情节，并构建四种不同类型基线模型（含图感知模型）以刻画难度特征，同时通过逻辑无关基线和乱序控制实验进行生成器一致性检验。

Result: 所有基线模型（包括表现最好的图感知模型）在组合性留出集上均出现0.13至0.17的macro-F1性能差距，无法弥合；逻辑无关基线仍以高于内在数据频率的方式违反留出语义规则；乱序对照实验中macro-F1变化仅落在种子变异范围内，验证了生成器的一致性。

Conclusion: 该基准所揭示的组合性留出差距是结构性属性而非模型缺陷，框架设计的规则解耦策略有效规避了循环监督。生成的本体、转移模型及生成器均将开源，支持基准的重生成与扩展。

Abstract: Recognizing human behavior across levels of abstraction, from atomic actions to long-horizon intentions, requires data annotated along a semantic hierarchy. Large corpora provide isolated, atomically labeled clips without temporal composition, whereas recorded composite-activity corpora offer shallow, domain-narrow, fixedhierarchies. A benchmark-generation and evaluation frameworkis proposed that synthesizes a four-level hierarchical-intention benchmark, spanning actions, activities, low-level intentions (LLIs), and high-level intentions (HLIs), from a flat single-label action corpus while retaining real pre-extracted features at the action level. Episodes are assembled by a transition model under a subject-consistency constraint, and a coverage-aware sampler reduces the subject usage Gini from 0.566 to 0.248. Synthesizing such a benchmark raises a circular-supervision risk that recorded datasets avoid: if the rules generating the episodes also govern the evaluation, models can succeed by recovering the generator rather than through genuine reasoning. Validity is addressed by design, holding sequence-generation rules disjoint from the first-order-logic rules used at evaluation. The instantiation yields 15,002 episodes. Four reference baselines from different model families characterize difficulty, not as recognition methods. A compositional held-out gap of 0.13 to 0.17 macro-F1 appears across all baselines, including a graph-aware model that recognizes best yet does not close the gap, indicating a structural property of the benchmark rather than a model artifact. A logic-free baseline still violates the held-out semantic rules above their intrinsic data rate, and the order-destroying control changes macro-F1 within seed variation, serving as a generator-consistency check. Theontology, transition model, and generator are released so the benchmark can beregenerated and extended.

</details>


### [241] [SkillLens: Visual Skill Cards for Retrieval-Augmented GUI Action Prediction and On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10775)
*Zhou Liu,Ligang Huang,Zeli Su,Zewei Pan,Zhaoyang Han,Xing Chen,Yuanfeng Song,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Visual Skill Cards（VSCs），一种状态条件化的记忆表示，将可复用过程与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号绑定，用于提升GUI智能体的视觉过程记忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算机操控智能体缺乏视觉过程记忆——它们能识别单个控件，但无法识别当前处于哪个熟悉的工作流、下一步该操作哪个控件、以及什么证据能确认进展。原始交互轨迹作为条件过长且噪声大，而纯文本技能描述又丢失了使过程适用的视觉状态信息。

Method: 提出SkillLens框架：(1) Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card方法从异构交互经验中构建VSCs，将过程与适用性线索、视觉证据和验证信号绑定；(2) 推理时检索相关卡片，选择性展开冻结的视觉语言模型执行器所需的证据进行GUI动作预测。此外提出CardDistill，将VSC证据作为特权教师上下文训练学生模型，使其无需运行时卡片检索即可行动。

Result: 在Multimodal-Mind2Web和WebLINX-BrowserGym两个基准上，SkillLens将冻结的GPT-5.4-mini执行器的Step SR提升+11.6点、Overall提升+2.9点；CardDistill进一步将对应的学生模型Qwen3-VL-2B的指标分别提升+12.0和+3.2点。

Conclusion: VSC为GUI智能体提供了一种有效的状态条件化记忆表示，能显著提升动作预测性能。SkillLens在推理时利用检索增强、CardDistill在训练时蒸馏VSC知识，两种范式均验证了该表示的实用价值。

Abstract: Computer-using agents can perceive rich software interfaces, yet their decisions often lack visual procedural memory: they may recognize individual controls without identifying which familiar workflow is active, which control matters next, or what evidence would confirm progress. Raw interaction traces preserve such information but are long and noisy to condition on, whereas text-only skills often omit the visual state that makes a procedure applicable. We introduce Visual Skill Cards (VSCs), a state-conditioned memory representation that binds reusable procedures with applicability cues, visual evidence, and verification signals. SkillLens constructs VSCs from heterogeneous interaction experience through Trace-to-Visual-Skill-Card and, at inference time, retrieves relevant cards and selectively expands only the evidence needed by a fixed visual-language model executor for grounded GUI action prediction. The same representation also supports CardDistill, which uses VSC evidence as privileged teacher context to train a student that acts without runtime card retrieval. Across Multimodal-Mind2Web and WebLINX-BrowserGym, SkillLens improves the frozen GPT-5.4-mini executor by +11.6 points in Step SR and +2.9 points in Overall, respectively; CardDistill further improves the corresponding student-only Qwen3-VL-2B metrics by +12.0 and +3.2 points.

</details>


### [242] [ChemWorld: Programmable Chemical Worlds for Controlled and Replayable Agent Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10792)
*Jiangjie Qiu,Yijun Li,Xiaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ChemWorld 是一个可编程的化学实验环境，它将可复用的过程与观测组件编译为可执行世界，分离公共实验接口与私有的化学/材料法则，支持精确重放、审计以及系统性地改变世界参数，为跨多样化学世界的自主实验研究提供受控且可复现的基础平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主化学研究亟需可反复行动、观测与适应的环境。物理实验室提供真实材料证据但成本高、难以精确复现对照干预；现有数字环境则大多将底层实验世界固定不变。因此，需要一种能够灵活改变世界规则、同时保持公共实验交互条件不变的可编程环境，以支持系统性对照研究。

Method: ChemWorld 将可复用的过程组件与观测组件编译为可执行世界，核心设计在于将面向智能体的公共实验契约与评估者私有的化学/材料法则解耦。支持世界组成与操作条件的改变，允许在固定公共任务与交互条件下单独变更隐藏法则。所有操作、失败、资源变化和状态转移均以事务方式记录，确保完整的环境-行动轨迹可精确重放与审计。进行了全量资格测试（参考注册表、52 种生成组合、模块/接口/编译/无效动作测试），并通过 8 个确定性实验案例和 6 对父子世界分叉实验验证了共享生命周期语义、失败恢复、精确重放及单条私有法则干预的隔离效应。

Result: 全量资格测试覆盖了参考注册表、52 种生成组合以及模块、接口、编译和无效动作测试。8 个确定性案例展示了共享生命周期语义、失败恢复和精确重放能力；6 对父子世界分叉实验在匹配的公共条件下成功隔离了单条私有法则变更的效应。一个独立智能体也通过相同的公共接口在非参考世界中完成了完整生命周期。

Conclusion: 在声明的组件与模型领域内，ChemWorld 提供了一个受控且可重放的实验基座，能够在系统变化的化学世界中研究实验行为，与物理实验室证据和校准形成互补。

Abstract: Autonomous chemistry increasingly depends on environments in which agents can repeatedly act, observe, and adapt.Physical laboratories provide essential real-material evidence but are costly to repeat and difficult to use for tightly matched interventions, whereas most digital environments keep the underlying experimental world largely fixed. We introduce ChemWorld, a programmable chemical environment in which reusable process and observation components are compiled into executable worlds. ChemWorld separates the public experimental contract available to an agent from evaluator-owned chemical and material laws. Researchers can therefore vary world composition and operating conditions, or change a single hidden law while holding the public task and interaction conditions fixed. Transactional execution records operations, failures, resource changes, and state transitions, allowing complete environment-action trajectories to be replayed exactly and audited. Full-census qualification covered the reference registry, 52 generated compositions, and module, interface, compilation, and invalid-action tests. Eight deterministic experimental cases demonstrated shared lifecycle semantics, failure recovery, and exact replay, while six parent-child world-fork pairs isolated the effects of single private-law interventions under matched public conditions. An independent agent also completed a full lifecycle in a non-reference world through the same public interface. Within the declared component and model domain, ChemWorld provides a controlled and replayable substrate for studying experimentation across systematically varied chemical worlds, complementary to physical-laboratory evidence and calibration.

</details>


### [243] [EvoMem: Memory-Augmented Evolution for Code Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10795)
*Viktor Volkov,Valentin Khrulkov,Andrey V. Galichin,Danil Sivtsov,Nikita Glazkov,Olga Volkova,Konstantin Pchelin,Iaroslav Bespalov,Dmitry V. Dylov,Petr Anokhin,Ivan Oseledets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvoMem 提出了一种面向LLM驱动进化程序搜索的持久记忆架构，通过捕获并复用成功的变异策略知识，减少重复探索，提升跨运行和跨任务的学习能力。在多个基准测试上，大多数评估场景显示目标指标或搜索速度有正向平均改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的进化框架在每次运行后丢弃了成功的变异策略中蕴含的可复用知识，导致相似思路被反复重新发现，错失了跨运行、跨任务知识迁移的机会。因此，需要一种机制来持久化并复用这些有价值的变异知识。

Method: EvoMem 采用两阶段持久记忆架构：(1) 提取与存储阶段——在每次进化运行结束后，将成功的变异事件转化为结构化的、任务感知的建议，并附带来源信息存入记忆库；(2) 检索与引导阶段——在后续进化过程中，根据当前任务和程序上下文，检索少量相关指令来指导变异操作。记忆内容以任务感知的"建议"形式组织，支持跨任务的知识迁移。

Result: 在几何优化、多跳问答、GPU内核优化及相关基准测试上，EvoMem 在大多数评估设置中展示了目标指标或搜索速度的正向平均改善，同时揭示了不同任务间的效果差异性。

Conclusion: EvoMem 证明了持久记忆机制能够减少LLM驱动进化搜索中的冗余探索，提升成功策略的复用与适应能力，为进化程序搜索中的知识积累和迁移提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Successful mutation strategies in evolutionary code search may contain reusable knowledge that is useful beyond a single run, and in some cases may transfer across related tasks and domains. However, existing LLM-driven evolutionary frameworks largely discard such knowledge, repeatedly rediscovering similar ideas and limiting opportunities for cross-run and cross-task learning. We introduce EvoMem, a persistent memory architecture for LLM-based evolutionary program search that captures and reuses candidate mutation knowledge. EvoMem converts successful mutation events into structured, task-aware advice for future runs. It operates in two phases: after each run, it extracts and stores promising ideas with provenance, and during subsequent evolution, it retrieves a small set of relevant instructions based on the current task and program context to guide mutation. Across geometric optimization, multi-hop question answering, GPU kernel optimization, and related benchmarks, our experiments show positive average improvements in target metrics or search speed for most evaluated settings, while also revealing variability across tasks. Overall, EvoMem provides evidence that persistent memory can reduce some redundant exploration and improve the reuse and adaptation of successful strategies in LLM-driven evolutionary search.

</details>


### [244] [Hypothesis Frontier: Verifier Guided LLM and Symbolic Search for First-Order Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10843)
*Serafim Batzoglou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Hypothesis Frontier 是一种神经符号框架，通过验证器引导的迭代式假设生成与精化，结合符号修正和精确简化，在保持训练预测不变的前提下，显著提升一阶概念合成问题的求解率并压缩最终公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一阶概念合成需要在多个有限关系结构上推断出一个一致的分类公式。虽然候选公式可精确评估，但量化一阶公式的搜索空间巨大，且LLM生成的公式往往语义上有前景却不够完全正确。现有方法缺乏有效的验证反馈闭环和符号层面的精确修正与简化机制。

Method: 提出 Hypothesis Frontier 框架：(1) 将LLM生成的每个公式在所有训练对象上精确评估；(2) 跨轮次保留最强的已验证假设，利用剩余错误引导后续生成；(3) 符号处理模块修复无效公式，同时保持对LLM生成假设的锚定；(4) 对训练有效的公式进行精确简化，保证所有训练预测不变。整体流程在匹配的模型、问题集和LLM轮次预算下进行对比实验。

Result: 在相同模型、问题集和LLM轮次预算下，Hypothesis Frontier 比重复使用原始提示生成的方法解决了显著更多的问题。最终公式经过精确简化后，许多训练有效的公式在保持所有训练预测不变的前提下被显著缩短。

Conclusion: 精确的符号推理不仅有助于解决更多的归纳问题，还能压缩大量结果公式。验证器引导的神经符号框架有效结合了LLM的语义生成能力和符号系统的精确验证与修正能力。

Abstract: First-order concept synthesis asks a system to infer one formula that classifies labeled objects consistently across several finite relational structures. Every candidate can be evaluated exactly, but quantified first-order formulas form a vast search space, and LLM outputs are often semantically promising without being fully correct. We introduce Hypothesis Frontier, a verifier-guided neurosymbolic framework that evaluates each LLM formula on every training object, retains the strongest verified hypothesis across rounds, and uses its remaining errors to guide subsequent generation. Symbolic processing repairs invalid formulas while remaining anchored to the LLM-generated hypothesis, and simplifies train-valid formulas without changing any training prediction. Under matched models, problem sets, and LLM-round budgets, Hypothesis Frontier solves substantially more problems than repeated original-prompt generation. After the final formulas are selected, exact simplification shortens many train-valid formulas while preserving every training prediction. Exact symbolic reasoning therefore helps both to solve more induction problems and to compress many of the resulting formulas.

</details>


### [245] [Enhanced Filtering Algorithms for the Euclidean Traveling Salesperson Problem and its variants in Constraint Logic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10881)
*Alessandro Bertagnon,Marco Gavanelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对欧几里得旅行商问题（Euclidean TSP），提出在约束逻辑编程（CLP）框架下利用节点坐标的几何信息设计新型过滤算法，实现了比传统基于完整距离矩阵方法更强的约束传播，并将该方法扩展至欧几里得广义旅行商问题（EGTSP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有约束编程文献处理欧几里得TSP时，通常先计算完整距离矩阵再按一般情形处理，完全忽略了点坐标本身蕴含的几何信息，导致约束传播不够强，求解效率受限。

Method: 在约束逻辑编程（CLP）框架下，利用点的二维坐标几何信息，设计新的过滤/约束传播算法；并进一步将该方法推广至欧几里得广义旅行商问题（EGTSP）。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的方法相比现有方法具有显著的计算优势。

Conclusion: 利用几何信息增强约束传播是解决欧几里得TSP及其变体的有效途径，相比传统忽略坐标信息的方法，能显著提升求解性能，并具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) is one of the best-known problems in computer science and arises in many engineering applications, such as smart vehicles and intelligent transportation systems. In the "Euclidean" case, each node is defined by its coordinates in the plane and distances are computed using the Euclidean metric. In the Constraint Programming (CP) literature, the Euclidean TSP is typically addressed by computing the full distance matrix and treating it as a general case; however this approach ignores the geometric information carried by the points' coordinates. In this work, we propose new filtering algorithms, implemented in Constraint Logic Programming (CLP), that exploit such geometric information to achieve stronger constraint propagation than existing approaches. Moreover, we show how this methodology can be extended to other Euclidean variants of the TSP, including the Euclidean Generalized Traveling Salesperson Problem (EGTSP), which is relevant in practical routing and logistics applications. Experimental results demonstrate the computational advantages of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [246] [ComBodied Agents: a New Paradigm of Human-Centric Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10915)
*Qianggang Ding,Xingyao Wang,Rui Feng,Zhibin Wang,Feixiang Wang,Kelong Mao,Hao Sun,Zhiyao Luo,Jiankai Tang,Lei Li,Jiadong Guo,Minheng Ni,Weicong Lin,Chenxi Yang,Hongxiang Gao,Zhenghua Chen,Yang Bai,Min Wu,Jun Cheng,Huazhu Fu,Dacheng Tao,Bang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"组合具身智能体"（Combodied Agents）这一人本中心范式，旨在弥合数字智能体（仅操作软件状态）与具身智能体（仅操作物理状态）之间的结构性鸿沟。该范式以人的状态和能动性为核心，通过感知-建模-预测-干预的闭环，持续追踪并支持个体的状态轨迹，将软件工具、传感器、可穿戴设备、机器人和人工服务视为行动渠道而非终极目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic AI存在结构性缺陷：数字智能体仅能变换软件状态，具身智能体仅能变换物理状态，两者均未将人的动态状态和能动性作为建模、干预与评估的核心对象。例如，老年人漏服药物时，现有智能体只能发送提醒或递送药物，却无法判断遗忘、困惑、副作用还是故意拒绝等根本原因，也无法提供适配的支持。

Method: 提出Combodied Agents框架，包含四个核心组件的闭环：1）基于事件的多模态感知，重构有意义的个人事件；2）纵向可纠正记忆提供时序上下文；3）个人世界模型（Personal World Models）估计不同决策和干预下的未来个人状态与结果；4）可容许干预策略（admissible intervention policy）在知情同意、不确定性、安全性、可逆性和用户控制约束下选择相称的支持措施。该框架采用目的限定、不确定性感知、用户可纠正的表征，而非详尽的人类数字孪生。还规划了设计空间（人状态目标、关系情境、智能体角色），并提出场景中心化评估、能动性保持度量、基准需求、边缘原生个人模型和治理方向。

Result: 论文构建了Combodied Agents的概念框架与设计空间，统一了个人助理、健康智能体、AI伴侣和自适应人-AI系统等碎片化能力，将其整合为以人的状态轨迹为中心的闭环系统。提出了场景中心化评估体系、能动性保持度量指标、边缘原生个人模型的技术路径，以及相应的治理建议。

Conclusion: Combodied Agents将Agentic AI从外部任务完成转向持续的人类福祉增益，代表了一种从以任务为中心到以人为中心的范式转换。该框架为构建真正理解、预测并支持个体长期状态演化的智能系统提供了理论基础和设计蓝图。

Abstract: After an older adult misses a medication dose, a software agent can send another reminder and an embodied agent can bring the medication. Yet neither explains whether the person forgot, is confused, has side effects, or deliberately refused, nor what support is appropriate. This reveals a structural gap in Agentic AI: Digital Agents primarily transform software states, while Embodied Agents transform physical states; neither makes a person's evolving state and agency the primary object of modeling, intervention, and evaluation. We introduce Combodied Agents, a human-centered paradigm that perceives, models, predicts, and supports individual human-state trajectories over time, using software tools, sensors, wearables, robots, and human services as action channels rather than end goals. We unify fragmented capabilities across personal assistants, health agents, AI companions, and adaptive human--AI systems into a closed loop: event-based multimodal perception reconstructs meaningful personal events; longitudinal, correctable memory provides temporal context; Personal World Models estimate future personal states and outcomes under alternative decisions and interventions; and an admissible intervention policy selects proportionate support under consent, uncertainty, safety, reversibility, and user control. Feedback from the person and environment updates the loop. Rather than requiring an exhaustive Human Digital Twin, the framework uses purpose-bounded, uncertainty-aware, user-correctable representations. We organize the design space by human-state targets, relational contexts, and agent roles, and propose scenario-centered evaluation, agency-preservation metrics, benchmark requirements, edge-native personal models, and governance directions. Combodied Agents shift Agentic AI from external task completion toward sustained human benefit.

</details>


### [247] [IO Factory: Simulating AI-Enabled Influence Campaigns at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10920)
*Lukasz Olejnik,Wenchao Dong,Jonas R. Kunst,Signe Riemer-Sørensen,Tobias Herb,Meeyoung Cha,Daniel Thilo Schroeder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IO Factory框架，一个用于模拟信息与影响力活动的AI驱动系统，支持从规划到适应的全流程可追溯分析，可扩展至10万个智能体，用于再现性研究和红队分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字操纵的威胁已从单一语言模型的劝说性文本升级为"AI蜂群"——即持续协作的智能体群体，能够根据平台反馈自适应调整，并将有组织活动伪装成普通社交互动。由于此类活动无法从孤立消息中识别，需跨越规划、平台行动、曝光、解读、测量与适应的连续谱系进行整体分析。

Method: 构建IO Factory框架，在受控模拟平台内表示完整的影响力活动流程：将参与者角色、平台行动、曝光记录、基于模型的结构化评估及模拟人群中配置的信念变化整合为一体。框架记录每次运行中的参与者、目标、行动约束、曝光路径和测量规则。

Result: 在最多10万个智能体的配置规模下进行实验评估，结果表明IO Factory能够规模化执行活动时间线，并产出可检查的曝光证据以及配置信念变量中可测量的变化。

Conclusion: IO Factory通过记录完整的活动要素，支持协调性影响力的再现性研究和红队分析，为对抗AI蜂群式操纵提供了系统化的分析工具。

Abstract: We introduce IO Factory, an AI-driven framework for simulating information and influence campaigns as fully integrated, traceable processes. The threat of digital manipulation now extends beyond persuasive text from individual language models to AI swarms, i.e., persistent groups of coordinated agents that adapt to platform feedback and disguise organized campaigns as ordinary social interaction. Because such campaigns cannot be identified from isolated messages alone, they must be analyzed across a continuous spectrum of planning, platform action, exposure, interpretation, measurement, and adaptation. IO Factory represents this process inside a controlled simulated platform, linking actor roles, platform actions, exposure records, structured model-based evaluations, and configured changes in the simulated population. We implement the architecture and evaluate it across configurations of up to 100,000 agents. The results show that IO Factory executes campaign timelines at scale and produces inspectable evidence of exposure and measured movement in configured belief variables. By recording the actors, objectives, action constraints, exposure paths, and measurement rules used in each run, IO Factory supports reproducible research and red-team analysis of coordinated influence.

</details>


### [248] [ThinkRetrieve: Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning Traces for Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10928)
*Vaibhav Singh,Soumya Suvra Ghosal,Sarvesh Gharat,Soumyabrata Pal,Ramasuri Narayanam,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ThinkRetrieve 是一种测试时扩展框架，通过在推理的每一步动态检索已解决的示例并注入思维链，为模型提供"如何推理"的引导，从而克服传统序列测试时扩展中随推理链增长而出现的收益递减和误差累积问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模推理模型（LRMs）通过增加推理时的计算量来生成长思维链以提升性能，但近期研究发现，序列化测试时扩展往往带来收益递减甚至负收益，原因在于更长的推理链会引入更高的不确定性、误差累积以及偏离原始问题。因此需要一种方法在扩展推理时既能利用额外计算，又能避免上述退化问题。

Method: 提出 ThinkRetrieve 框架：维护一个包含问题及其逐步解答的外部语料库；在 LRM 推理的每一个中间步骤，动态检索与当前推理状态最相关的已解决示例，并将其直接注入思维链中，为模型提供推理方式的引导（而非仅提供相关事实）。在 GSM-8K、MATH-500、AIME 2025 和 SciQ 等基准上，对 1.5B 至 8B 参数的五种推理模型进行实验评估。

Result: ThinkRetrieve 在所有测试基准和模型上均一致地提升了标准测试时扩展的准确率，在 AIME 2025 上实现了高达 60% 的相对提升。

Conclusion: 通过在每一步推理中动态检索并注入相关示例，ThinkRetrieve 有效地引导模型进行推理，克服了传统测试时扩展的退化问题，显著提升了 LRMs 的推理性能，为测试时扩展提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) improve performance by allocating additional inference-time compute to generate extended chain-of-thought reasoning. However, recent studies reveal that sequential test-time scaling often yields diminishing or even negative returns, as longer traces exhibit increased uncertainty, error compounding, and drift from the original problem. We propose ThinkRetrieve, a test-time scaling framework that augments the reasoning traces of LRMs with dynamically retrieved solved examples at each reasoning step. Given an external corpus of problems paired with step-by-step solutions, ThinkRetrieve retrieves relevant exemplars at each intermediate step and injects them directly into the thinking trace, providing the model with guidance on how to reason rather than merely what facts are relevant. Experiments across five reasoning models (1.5B--8B parameters) on GSM-8K, MATH-500, AIME 2025, and SciQ demonstrate that ThinkRetrieve consistently improves accuracy over standard test-time scaling, with relative gains of up to $60\%$ on AIME 2025.

</details>


### [249] [FedCGR: Federated Cross-Domain Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10929)
*Zhuodong Liu,Hugen Lv,Xiangyu Li,Bohan Guo,Peiyu Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedCGR，一种联邦跨域推荐框架，通过将物品表示为基于公共元数据的离散语义ID（SID）序列来实现跨域对齐，同时引入可靠性感知的语义接口和原型个性化生成器来解决语义瓶颈和负迁移问题，在多个Amazon跨域场景中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦跨域推荐中，行为锚点（如重叠用户、共享交互信号）稀疏或不可用，导致跨域对齐困难。现有联邦SID生成器面临两个约束：SID分词器须固定以保证token一致性，造成语义瓶颈（本地CF信号无法全局共享）；联邦平均在域异质性下产生负迁移。

Method: 提出FedCGR框架：(1) 利用公共物品侧元数据将物品表示为离散语义ID序列，通过共享词汇表实现跨域对齐；(2) 设计可靠性感知语义接口（reliability-aware semantic interface）注入本地协同过滤证据；(3) 训练原型个性化生成器（prototype-personalized generator），根据域相关性选择性聚合共享参数，同时保持域特定量本地化。

Result: 在六个Amazon跨域场景上的实验表明，FedCGR在full-ranking和sampled evaluation协议下均一致优于联邦生成基线方法，并取得与强序列推荐和联邦CDR方法相当甚至更优的性能。

Conclusion: 通过稳定的语义物品语言和显式适配机制，FedCGR有效解决了联邦CDR中的跨域对齐、语义瓶颈和负迁移问题，为隐私保护下的跨域推荐提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cross-domain recommendation (CDR) transfers preference knowledge across related domains, but federated deployment makes cross-domain alignment difficult because the behavioral anchors that align item spaces, such as overlapping users and shared interaction signals, are often sparse, unavailable, or privacy-sensitive across clients. To address this tension, we revisit federated CDR as generation over a stable semantic item language. By representing items as discrete semantic ID (SID) sequences derived from public item-side metadata, cross-domain item alignment is induced by a shared vocabulary rather than by exchanging private interactions or aligning domain-specific embeddings. Directly federating SID-based generators, however, introduces two design constraints: the SID tokenizer must remain fixed to preserve cross-client token consistency, which creates a semantic-only bottleneck because local collaborative filtering (CF) signals cannot be globally shared or aligned; meanwhile, standard federated averaging can cause negative transfer under domain heterogeneity. To overcome these constraints, we propose FedCGR, a federated generative CDR framework that keeps the item language stable and makes adaptation explicit. FedCGR injects local CF evidence through a reliability-aware semantic interface and trains a prototype-personalized generator that selectively aggregates shared parameters according to domain relatedness while keeping domain-specific quantities local. Experiments on six Amazon cross-domain scenarios show that FedCGR consistently outperforms federated generative baselines and achieves competitive performance against strong sequential and federated CDR methods under both full-ranking and sampled evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [250] [XCoT-VLA: Executable Chain-of-Thought for Vision-Language-Action Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10976)
*Foundation Model Team,XPeng Inc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: XCoT-VLA 提出用可执行的紧凑 token 替代冗长的自然语言链式思维，通过自动构建的 Reason-Action 监督学习，将驾驶推理压缩为 2-6 个 token，直接连接轨迹生成，在保持实时性的同时显著提升了自动驾驶规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型使用冗长的自然语言链式思维（CoT）进行推理，但这种方式存在三个核心问题：(1) 开放式生成，解码成本高；(2) 难以优化为面向动作的表示；(3) 不适合实时控制场景。因此需要一种紧凑、可执行且能直接驱动轨迹生成的推理表示。

Method: 提出 XCoT-VLA，核心包括：(1) 用自动构建的 Reason-Action 监督信号学习紧凑的可执行 CoT token，替代自然语言推理；(2) XCoT 序列通过共享的多模态自注意力与固定轨迹查询交互；(3) 确定性 token-函数路由机制——Reason FFN 处理 XCoT token，Control FFN 处理轨迹查询，采用 flow-matching 生成轨迹；(4) 进一步提出 XCoT Policy Optimization (XCPO) 作为在相同可执行 token 空间中的可选策略优化扩展。

Result: 在通用分布测试集上，纵向 ADE 从 1.645 降至 1.323；在换道场景中，横向 FDE 从 1.616 降至 0.648。推理仅需 2-6 个 XCoT token，大幅降低了自回归推理开销，保持在实时规划预算内。

Conclusion: 驾驶导向的推理可以被压缩为紧凑、可执行的 token 表示，并直接与轨迹生成连接，在保持实时性的同时实现显著的性能提升，为自动驾驶中的 VLA 模型部署提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models can connect scene understanding, semantic reasoning, and trajectory generation for autonomous driving. However, verbose natural-language Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is poorly suited to real-time control because it is open-ended, costly to decode, and difficult to optimize as an action-facing representation. We propose XCoT-VLA, which replaces descriptive rationales with compact executable CoT tokens learned from automatically constructed Reason-Action supervision. Logged trajectories provide action evidence, while scene context supplies causal semantics. The predicted XCoT sequence remains in context and conditions fixed trajectory queries through shared multimodal self-attention. Deterministic token-function routing applies the Reason FFN to XCoT tokens and the Control FFN to trajectory queries for flow-matching trajectory generation. We further introduce XCoT Policy Optimization (XCPO) as an optional refinement extension in the same executable token space. XCoT-VLA reduces longitudinal ADE from 1.645 to 1.323 on a general-distribution set and lateral FDE from 1.616 to 0.648 in lane-change scenarios. By representing driving-oriented reasoning with only 2-6 executable XCoT tokens, our method substantially reduces autoregressive reasoning overhead and remains within the real-time planning budget. These results demonstrate that driving-oriented reasoning can be compact, executable, and directly connected to trajectory generation.

</details>


### [251] [V-FiLLM: Verified Financial LLM Reasoning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11047)
*Alicia Larsen,Victoire Laurent,Aulia Kharis Rakhamsari,Lara Turgut,Nino Antulov-Fantulin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出V-FiLLM框架，通过可执行计算树从真实表格自动生成金融推理基准，无需模型标注，可任意扩展并独立控制四个难度维度。实验表明推理深度增加时准确率下降高达51%，数值扰动下下降47个百分点；LoRA微调可将准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM基准在STEM领域取得进展，但基于结构化数据的金融推理评估仍不充分。传统人工标注成本高且引入标注误差，需要一种可扩展、低成本且答案天然正确的基准生成方法。

Method: V-FiLLM框架从真实表格构建可执行计算树，通过符号求值获得真实答案，再渲染为自然语言问题，完全移除模型参与标注。框架支持四个独立可控的难度轴：计算深度、表达式广度、金融概念复杂度和上下文规模。

Result: 在开源模型上评估发现：(1)推理深度增加导致准确率下降高达51%；(2)对抗性数值扰动下准确率下降达47个百分点；(3)对验证后的思维链轨迹进行轻量级LoRA微调，在留出问题上准确率从81.1%提升至85.6%，在FinQA上超越基模型5个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化表格上的鲁棒金融推理仍是重大挑战；基于验证思维链的轻量级LoRA微调是提升组合推理能力的有前景方向，为金融QA领域提供了低成本适配路径。

Abstract: While existing benchmarks have made substantial progress in evaluating LLMs across STEM domains, financial reasoning over structured data remains comparatively less explored. We introduce V-FiLLM, a framework that generates financial reasoning benchmarks from executable computation trees grounded in real tables, yielding items whose answers are correct by construction. Trees are evaluated symbolically to obtain ground truth and rendered into natural-language questions, removing any model from the labeling loop, so items can be generated at arbitrary scale without annotation cost and without inheriting a generator's error rate. V-FiLLM exposes four independently controllable axes of difficulty including computation depth, expression breadth, financial concept complexity, and context size. By evaluating on open-source models, we find that accuracy falls up to 51% as reasoning depth increases, and up to 47% points under adversarial numerical perturbations, highlighting remaining challenges in robust financial reasoning over tables. We further show that lightweight LoRA fine-tuning on verified chain-of-thought traces improves accuracy from 81.1% to 85.6% on held-out problems and outperforms the base model by 5% points on FinQA (Chen et al., 2022a), s), suggesting that targeted, low-cost adaptation is a promising direction for compositional reasoning in financial QA.

</details>


### [252] [SkillZip: Evaluation-Free Skill Compression for Self-Evolving Agents by Discovering Reusable Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11079)
*Xiaofan Bai,Hongqiang Lin,Chao Liu,Yantao Zhang,Xuan Jin,Xipeng Cao,Yuhong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillZip 提出一种无评估的智能体技能压缩方法，通过寻找技能的最短忠实结构解释来实现压缩。其核心思想是"解释一次，引用多次"——将重复规则在适用范围内声明一次，将重复动作序列提取为共享过程，仅保留差异作为显式异常。该方法基于带类型的最大描述长度目标，配合硬覆盖约束，支持一次性压缩和持续写入两种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自进化智能体在积累可复用技能时，同一需求常被多次重复表述，共同动作序列被复制而非复用，导致技能注入成本高、维护困难。通用提示压缩不适用于此场景，因为技能并非扁平段落，而是包含名称、描述、工作流、工具合约、输出合约和罕见异常等结构化元素。现有评估引导的压缩方法引入 rollout 成本和对压缩时评估集的依赖。

Method: SkillZip 将技能压缩形式化为带类型的最大描述长度（MDL）目标，在技能合约和残差上施加硬覆盖约束，覆盖所有提取的触发器、工作流边、工具需求、义务和输出字段。提供简单共享阈值，通过构造保留独特的罕见规则，支持高效局部更新。提供两种模式：(1) 一次性模式，通过一次结构化提取调用和确定性优化完成；(2) 持续 Zip-on-Write 模式，集成每次自进化补丁时无需重放任务或重新解析完整历史。

Result: 通过全面的实验评估，SkillZip 在压缩性能、泛化能力和成本开销方面均展现出有效性和优越性。

Conclusion: SkillZip 作为一种无需评估的结构化技能压缩方法，通过 MDL 目标和硬覆盖约束，实现了对自进化智能体技能的高效压缩，在保持忠实性的同时显著降低技能维护成本，且支持持续增量更新。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents accumulate reusable skills by appending successful procedures and failure fixes. Over time, the same requirement is often restated in several branches, examples, and warnings, while common action sequences are copied rather than reused. The resulting skill becomes expensive to inject and difficult to maintain. Generic prompt compression is ill-suited to this setting because a skill is not a flat passage: its name and description define when it applies, its workflow controls execution, its tool and output contracts constrain validity, and rare exceptions may remain essential even when no sampled task activates them. Evaluation-guided compression can test these behaviors, but it introduces rollouts, cost, and dependence on the compression-time evaluation set. We present SkillZip, an evaluation-free method that compresses a skill by finding its shortest faithful structural explanation. The intuition is explain once, reference many: state a repeated rule once at the scope where it applies, factor a repeated action sequence into a shared procedure, and keep only the differences as explicit exceptions. We formalize this intuition as a typed minimum description-length objective over a skill contract and a residual, subject to a hard coverage constraint for every extracted trigger, workflow edge, tool requirement, obligation, and output field. The formulation provides simple sharing thresholds, preserves unique rare rules by construction, and supports efficient local updates. SkillZip has a one-shot mode with one structured extraction call and deterministic optimization, and a continual Zip-on-Write mode that integrates each self-evolution patch without replaying tasks or reparsing the full history. Through comprehensive experimental evaluations, we demonstrate the effectiveness and superiority of SkillZip in compression performance, generalizability, and cost overhead.

</details>


### [253] [RTSKG: Building a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11080)
*Shutong Zhu,Tianxing Wu,Runfeng Liu,Yuang Gu,Xuan He,Yuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个轨道交通站知识图谱（RTSKG）数据集，通过统一模式显式建模城市实体间的空间和语义交互，以链接数据形式发布，并在站点区域商铺推荐和知识增强的客流量预测任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前城市级轨道交通站相关研究（如客流量预测）在数据组织层面往往忽略了各类城市实体之间的复杂交互关系，缺乏能够显式建模多源异构城市实体间空间与语义关联的数据集。

Method: 作者构建了RTSKG知识图谱数据集，将轨道交通站、道路路段、兴趣点（POI）等异构城市实体，通过专门设计的统一模式（unified schema）进行整合，建模其空间和语义交互，并以Linked Data形式发布（https://w3id.org/rtskg/）。

Result: 在站点区域商铺推荐和知识增强的客流量预测两项任务上的评估结果表明，RTSKG能够有效提升任务性能，展现了其支持城市级轨道交通站分析的潜力。

Conclusion: RTSKG数据集通过知识图谱显式建模城市实体间的交互关系，为城市级轨道交通站相关任务提供了有价值的数据基础设施，未来有望在更多城市场景分析中发挥作用。

Abstract: Rail transit systems play a vital role in urban mobility and economic development. As key components of such systems, rail transit stations function as critical transport hubs that enhance urban accessibility and stimulate development in surrounding areas. City-level rail transit station related tasks (e.g., ridership prediction) require large-scale urban data, but current studies often neglect complex interactions among various urban entities in terms of data organization. In this paper, to address the above issue, we build a Rail Transit Station Knowledge Graph (RTSKG) dataset which explicitly models the spatial and semantic interactions among different kinds of urban entities, to benefit city-level rail transit station related tasks. RTSKG integrates heterogeneous urban entities, such as rail transit stations, road segments, and points of interest, with a specially designed unified schema, and is accessible as Linked Data at https://w3id.org/rtskg/. Evaluations on station-area store recommendation and knowledge-enhanced ridership prediction demonstrate the effectiveness of RTSKG, highlighting its potential to support city-level rail transit station analysis.

</details>


### [254] [Why Does CLAUDE.md Keep Growing? Catastrophic Remembering in Agentic Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11095)
*Kushal Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 代理编码文档（如CLAUDE.md）在实际仓库中会无限膨胀，根源在于"灾难性记忆"——追加指令成本低，但删除指令却因遗忘其存在理由而面临组合爆炸式风险。本文通过分析1867个仓库中24.7万条指令生命周期验证了这一现象，并提出用"提示注释"编码潜在推理来遏制膨胀，可将多余指令减少99.3%，并在真实场景中提升指令遵循能力达23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理编码工作流中，用于指导AI agent的提示文件（如CLAUDE.md）会持续追加指令而无限制增长，最终导致上下文窗口膨胀和性能退化。现有持续学习领域关注的是"灾难性遗忘"（catastrophic forgetting），而本文指出存在一个被忽视的对称问题——"灾难性记忆"（catastrophic remembering）：由于删除指令的成本与指令集合大小呈指数关系（O(2^|D|)），开发者倾向于只追加不删除，导致提示无限膨胀。

Method: 首先，在1867个仓库中对247,694条指令生命周期进行大规模实证分析，量化提示增长模式、净增指令速率，以及指令存续时间与删除概率的关系（通过log-hazard模型）。其次，提出解决方案：对IFEval基准进行反演，构造"可验证世界"（verifiable worlds），其中最优提示已知，证明在提示中嵌入编码潜在推理的注释可将多余指令减少99.3%。最后，将此方法推广至WildIFEval真实场景，验证注释对指令遵循能力的提升效果。

Result: 实证分析显示：代理提示在生命周期中增长超过三倍（+226%），每次提交净增4.9条指令；指令存活越久，被删除的概率越低（log-hazard = -0.032/commit）。在可验证世界中，提示注释消除了99.3%的多余指令（增长率从+211.3%降至+1.4%）。在WildIFEval真实场景中，提示注释使指令遵循能力最多提升23.1%。

Conclusion: 灾难性记忆是代理编码系统中提示膨胀的根本原因，其机制与持续学习中的灾难性遗忘对称但方向相反。提示注释作为一种简单有效的干预手段，通过保留指令背后的潜在推理来打破"只追加"的恶性循环，既能在受控环境中近乎消除膨胀，又能在真实场景中显著提升性能。作者以"如果英语是新的代码，为什么我们还不用注释？"作结，呼吁将软件工程中的注释实践引入提示工程。

Abstract: Agentic coding READMEs like CLAUDE.md grow without bound in real repositories, stopping only when the repository retires or someone rewrites the file wholesale. We trace this to imperfect recall: appending an instruction is always cheap, but once an instruction's rationale is gone, deleting it without risking a correctness regression costs O(2^|D|) in a prompt of |D| instructions. We name the resulting divergence catastrophic remembering, the inverse of catastrophic forgetting around which continual learning is organized. First, we characterize this phenomenon across 247,694 instruction lifetimes in 1,867 repositories: agentic prompts grow without bound, more than tripling over their lifetime (+226%), gaining +4.9 net instructions every commit; further, the older an instruction gets, the less likely it is to be deleted (log-hazard -0.032/commit). Then, we show that prompt comments can halt the growth: inverting IFEval yields verifiable worlds whose optimal prompts are known, and there comments encoding latent reasoning remove 99.3% of excess instructions (+211.3% to +1.4%). Finally, applying the same inversion to WildIFEval, we show that prompt comments can improve real-world agentic instruction-following by up to 23.1%. If English is the new code, why don't we have comments yet?

</details>


### [255] [sLTN: Structural Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11136)
*Davide Rinaldi,Luciano Serafini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 sLTN，一种对逻辑张量网络（LTN）的扩展，将结构维度（如时间步、序列位置、图节点）提升为语言中的一等元素，使其能够直接表达时序、顺序和关系约束，并在逻辑层面进行显式量化与推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 LTN 主要适用于以扁平个体集合表示的数据，无法显式捕获时间顺序、序列位置或图连接等结构组织。为了在保持可微分学习的优势下，直接对具有内在结构的数据进行逻辑约束建模，需要扩展 LTN 的表达能力。

Method: 作者引入结构维度（structural dimensions）作为 sLTN 核心概念——即与领域特定组织关联的命名张量轴。sLTN 形式化定义了语法和模糊张量语义，允许对结构维度进行显式量化，并通过结构关系表达时序、序列和关系约束。该框架在 PyTorch 中实现，包含声明式签名、公式解析和张量化解释等组件，并在无结构维度时退化为原始 LTN 语义。

Result: sLTN 在代表性时序和序列推理示例上得到了验证；在未使用结构维度的情况下，其语义可完全恢复为原始 LTN。配套开源库 sltn 已发布在 GitHub。

Conclusion: sLTN 成功扩展了 LTN，使结构组织成为可量化、可关系化的第一类语言要素，为时序、序列和图结构数据的神经符号建模提供了统一且向后兼容的框架。

Abstract: Logic Tensor Networks (LTN) provide a neurosymbolic framework in which first-order logic is interpreted through tensor operations, enabling logical constraints to be integrated with differentiable learning. However, the original formulation of LTN is primarily suited to data represented as flat collections of individuals, and does not explicitly capture structural organization such as temporal order, sequential position, or graph connectivity. We introduce sLTN, an extension of LTN that makes structural dimensions first-class elements of the language. Structural dimensions represent named tensor axes associated with domain-specific organization, such as time steps, sequence positions, or graph nodes. They can be quantified explicitly, related through structural relations, and used to express temporal, sequential, and relational constraints directly at the logical level. We formalize the syntax and fuzzy tensor semantics of sLTN and show that, in the absence of structural dimensions, the framework recovers the original LTN semantics as a special case. We further describe a PyTorch implementation based on a declarative signature, formula parsing, and tensorial interpretation. The framework is illustrated on representative temporal and sequential reasoning examples. This paper serves as a companion to the sltn library, available at https://github.com/logictensornetworks/sltn.

</details>


### [256] [Long-Horizon AI Research for Grothendieck Constant: A Case Study in Human-AI Mathematical Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11195)
*Alan Li,Rahul Saha,Anton Xue,Swarat Chaudhuri,Adam Klivans,Pravesh K Kothari,Raghu Meka*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过一个案例研究，展示了AI系统如何辅助数学研究，成功改进了Grothendieck常数$K_G$的上下界，并详细讨论了AI在数学研究中的优势、局限及创造突破性洞见的理想条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在数学研究中应用日益广泛，但如何有效使用AI仍不明确。作者旨在通过具体案例，系统性地展示AI辅助数学研究的实践经验和最佳条件。

Method: 采用广泛的案例研究方法，使用AI研究系统对Grothendieck常数$K_G$的界进行改进。该系统能够产生被领域专家认为具有新颖性的洞见，最终将下界改进为$6π/11$，上界改进为$π/(2\log(1+\sqrt2)) - 10^{-4}$。

Result: 成功将Grothendieck常数$K_G$的最佳已知界收紧，得到了更精确的上下界范围：$\frac{6π}{11} \le K_G \le \frac{π}{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}$。这些改进由AI研究系统驱动完成。

Conclusion: AI能够在数学研究中产生领域专家认可的新颖洞见。成功的关键在于为AI创造理想的研究条件。作者还深入反思了AI在数学研究中的优势与不足。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used in mathematics research, but it is often unclear how to use them effectively. Towards this, we present an extensive case study of how AI was used to improve bounds on the Grothendieck constant $K_G$, which captures the hardness between combinatorial problems and their continuous relaxations. Specifically, while the precise value of $K_G$ is not known, we recently tightened the best known bounds to \[
  \frac{6π}{11}
  \;\le\;
  K_G
  \;\le\;
  \fracπ{2\log(1+\sqrt2)} - 10^{-4}. \] Crucially, these improvements were achieved using an AI research system that could arrive at insights deemed novel by domain experts. We give a detailed discussion of our experience using AI for mathematics research, particularly touching upon its strengths and weaknesses, as well as our experience with creating ideal conditions for AI to arrive at breakthrough insights.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [257] [Transformer Geometry Observatory TGO-IV: Developmental Topology Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09997)
*Kaustubh Kapil,Kishor P. Upla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TGO-IV 提出了一个基于持续同调的拓扑框架，通过构建 Vietoris-Rips 单纯复形并追踪其拓扑特征（如持久图、条形码图、Betti 曲线等）跨 Transformer 层的演化，系统分析表征点云的全局拓扑结构如何从前向传播中逐步发展为任务相关特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Transformer 可解释性研究大多聚焦于孤立层或整体网络的分析，缺乏对单个表征及其流形跨层发育演化的关注。本文旨在填补这一空白，追踪原始输入表征如何以及何时演变为任务相关的特征表征。

Method: 提出 TGO-IV 框架：从 token 级表征点云构建 Vietoris-Rips 单纯复形，利用持续同调提取拓扑不变量；采用持久图、条形码图、Betti 曲线、持久景观、Bottleneck 距离和 Wasserstein 距离等多种拓扑观测工具，全面分析表征点云全局拓扑结构在 Transformer 前向传播中的演化规律。

Result: （摘要中未报告具体实验结果。）

Conclusion: （摘要中未明确给出结论，核心贡献是提出了一套系统的拓扑分析框架，用于揭示 Transformer 表征的发育演化规律。）

Abstract: Transformers have had a profound impact on the world of language processing and computer vision. As efforts to answer the million-dollar question of ``How does a Transformer learn?" have been increasing, existing interpretability studies primarily analyze representations at isolated layers or the network as a whole, while the developmental evolution of individual representations and its manifolds across transformer layers remains underexplored. With this work, we aim at providing a comprehensive analysis of the evolution of representations as the representation point cloud transforms across the layers; thereby attempting to isolate layers or establish a trend which comes closer to justifying how and when raw input representations evolve into task-relevant feature representations. Thus, Transformer Geometry Observatory-TGO-IV introduces a topological framework for analysing the evolution of Transformer representations through the lens of Persistent Homology. Rather than studying local geometric properties alone, TGO-IV constructs Vietoris--Rips simplicial complexes from token-level representation point clouds and investigates the evolution of their persistent topological signatures across Transformer layers. The proposed framework comprises complementary topological observatories including Persistence Diagrams, Barcode Diagrams, Betti Curves, Persistence Landscapes, Bottleneck Distance, and Wasserstein Distance, enabling a comprehensive analysis of how the global topology of representation point clouds develops throughout the forward pass.

</details>


### [258] [Uncertainty-Aware Ensemble Deep Randomized Neural Networks for Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10007)
*M. Sajid,A. Quadir,A. Rahaman,P. N. Suganthan,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出直觉模糊深度RVFL（IF-dRVFL）和直觉模糊集成深度RVFL（IF-edRVFL）框架，通过引入直觉模糊理论，利用核空间中的隶属度和非隶属度自适应加权样本，增强模型对噪声和离群点的鲁棒性，在多个基准数据集上表现出优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SOTA深度随机化神经网络（dRVFL/edRVFL）对所有训练样本平等对待，未考虑样本质量差异。在含噪声和离群点的真实数据中，受污染特征会跨隐藏层传播，严重损害模型的鲁棒性和决策能力。亟需一种能有效区分干净样本、噪声和离群点的机制。

Method: 提出IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL框架，在核空间中引入直觉模糊理论，联合利用隶属度和非隶属度刻画样本。隶属度基于样本到其类别中心距离计算，非隶属度量化样本在局部邻域内的异质性。两者结合生成自适应权重，为干净样本分配高权重、噪声和离群点分配低权重，从而抑制污染特征在隐藏层间的传播。

Result: 在UCI和KEEL基准数据集上进行广泛实验，包括添加高斯噪声和不加噪声两种场景。实验结果表明，所提IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL模型显著优于现有SOTA模糊和非模糊方法。

Conclusion: 直觉模糊理论驱动的自适应样本加权机制能够有效提升深度随机化神经网络的鲁棒性。IF-dRVFL和IF-edRVFL在噪声环境下展现出显著的性能优势，代码已开源。

Abstract: The current state-of-the-art (SOTA) deep randomized neural networks, such as deep Random Vector Functional Link (dRVFL) and ensemble deep RVFL (edRVFL), treat all training samples uniformly, which limits their robustness and effectiveness when applied to real-world datasets containing noise and outliers. Furthermore, the propagation of contaminated features across hidden layers negatively influences the decision-making capability of these models. To overcome these limitations, we propose intuitionistic fuzzy dRVFL (IF-dRVFL) and intuitionistic fuzzy edRVFL (IF-edRVFL) frameworks that enhance model robustness. The proposed models unify intuitionistic fuzzy theory to exploit sample neighborhood information in the kernel space by jointly considering membership and non-membership degrees for each sample. Membership degrees are computed based on the distance of samples from their respective class centroids, while non-membership degrees quantify sample heterogeneity within local neighborhoods. These measures are employed to assign adaptive weights to training samples, enabling effective discrimination among clean, noisy, and outlier data points. Extensive experiments conducted on UCI and KEEL benchmark datasets, with and without the presence of Gaussian noise, demonstrate the superiority of the proposed IF-dRVFL and IF-edRVFL models over existing SOTA fuzzy and non-fuzzy approaches. The source code is available at https://github.com/mtanveer1/IF-edRVFL.

</details>


### [259] [CurveFP: Rational-Radix Logarithmic Datatypes with Closed Products for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10010)
*Ye Qiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CurveFP是一种闭合乘积码本族，通过交错对数曲线分布量化幅度，使得每个非零乘积在代数上闭合。乘积计算简化为符号异或和整数索引更新。实验表明，CurveFP 7位在4个7B-9B模型上优于张量级FP8困惑度，CurveFP 8位在全部36对GEMM比较中降低操作数NMSE，在3B token预训练中达到与BF16/FP8相当的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低精度数据类型大多优化标量保真度，但未改变其乘积引发的算术特性。作者希望设计一种从算术层面协同优化的数据类型，使乘积运算在代数上闭合且计算路径更简单。

Method: 提出CurveFP——一种闭合乘积码本族：在紧凑块尺度下，通过交错对数曲线分布量化幅度；有理基数调节动态范围与局部分辨率；统一曲线索引使每个非零乘积代数闭合；乘积退化为符号异或和整数索引更新；推导有限相位计数决定累加调度。实例化CurveFP eight (E4C3/E5C2)用于训练，CurveFP seven (E3C3)用于紧凑部署。

Result: CurveFP 7位在4个7B-9B模型上以少1个元素位的代价超越张量级FP8困惑度，与原生质量差距在1.32%以内；CurveFP 8位在所有36对前向/反向GEMM比较中降低操作数NMSE；3个匹配的128.3M参数模型完成3B token预训练，CurveFP 8位平均BF16推理困惑度22.5366 vs FP8的22.5407，且3个种子中格式引入的惩罚均更低；36单元下游矩阵中，CurveFP 8位训练的检查点在全部12个种子-格式比较中获得更低WikiText-103困惑度。

Conclusion: CurveFP实现了算术协同设计，兼具FP8级别的数值行为、7位推理能力和显著简化的乘积路径，在预训练困惑度和下游任务中表现优异或持平于FP8。

Abstract: Low-precision datatypes reduce language-model cost, but most formats optimize scalar fidelity while leaving the arithmetic induced by their products unchanged. We introduce CurveFP, a closed-product codebook family that distributes quantized magnitudes across interleaved logarithmic curves under compact block scales. A rational radix tunes dynamic range against local resolution, while uniform curve indices make every nonzero product algebraically closed. Product formation becomes an exact sign XOR and integer-index update, and a derived finite phase count determines the accumulation schedule. We instantiate this algebra as CurveFP eight E4C3/E5C2 for training and CurveFP seven E3C3 for compact deployment. In evaluation, CurveFP seven beats tensor-wise FP8 perplexity on four 7B--9B models with one fewer element bit and stays within 1.32\% of native quality. CurveFP eight lowers operand NMSE in all 36 paired forward and backward GEMM comparisons. Across three matched 128.3M-parameter triplets, every mode completes 3B-token pretraining per seed; CurveFP eight reaches mean BF16-inference perplexity 22.5366 versus 22.5407 for FP8 and incurs a lower format-induced penalty in all three seeds. A 36-cell downstream matrix finds lower WikiText-103 perplexity for the CurveFP eight-trained checkpoints in all 12 seed-format comparisons, with mixed PG-19 and task deltas. Together, these results establish CurveFP as an arithmetic co-design that combines FP8-class numerical behavior, seven-bit inference, and a substantially simpler product path.

</details>


### [260] [Sheaf-Based Federated Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10016)
*Gabriele D'Acunto,Enrico Grimaldi,Valeria Avino,Mario Edoardo Pandolfo,Leonardo Di Nino,Sergio Barbarossa,Paolo Di Lorenzo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SFRL 是一种基于层（sheaf）理论的联邦表示学习框架，通过可学习的层限制映射和拉普拉斯正则化，在无共享全局潜在空间的条件下实现异构智能体之间局部表示的对齐，并提出了交替优化的去中心化算法 Sheaf-FRL，具备收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构联邦系统中，各智能体在数据分布、传感模态、模型架构、潜在维度和局部学习目标上存在显著差异，现有方法多假设共享全局潜在空间，难以有效应对这些异构性。因此需要一种无需共享全局空间即可实现表示对齐的新框架。

Method: 提出 SFRL 框架：联合优化局部目标与基于可学习层限制映射的流形约束几何对齐正则项。通过正交变换和等距嵌入对齐相邻潜在表示，利用层拉普拉斯算子诱导的二次粘合正则项强制全局一致性。正则项仅在一小组共享引导样本上评估以保证可扩展性和通信效率。提出 Sheaf-FRL 去中心化算法：交替进行局部模型梯度更新与边限制映射的闭式 Procrustes 更新，并证明了算法在确定性和随机性设置下收敛到一阶驻点。

Result: 在语义通信场景下的协作分类任务中，Sheaf-FRL 在不同局部分布偏移程度下，局部分类精度和通信后分类精度均优于基线方法，并且在潜在空间维度压缩时表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SFRL 通过层理论的几何对齐机制，有效解决了异构联邦表示学习中的核心挑战，无需共享全局潜在空间即可实现全局一致性，实验验证了其优越的性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Heterogeneous federated systems require agents to learn and exchange informative representations despite differences in data distributions, sensing modalities, model architectures, latent dimensionalities, and local learning objectives. To address this challenge, we propose Sheaf-based Federated Representation Learning (SFRL), a general framework that jointly optimizes local objectives with a manifold-constrained geometric alignment regularizer based on learnable sheaf restriction maps. Unlike most existing approaches, SFRL does not assume a shared global latent space. Instead, global consistency emerges from the alignment of neighboring latent representations through orthogonal transformations and isometric embeddings. This alignment is enforced by a quadratic gluing regularizer induced by the sheaf Laplacian, whose learnable restriction maps adapt the geometry to the observed data. The penalty is evaluated on a small set of shared pilot samples, ensuring scalability and communication efficiency. We develop a decentralized algorithm for solving SFRL, termed Sheaf-FRL, which alternates between gradient updates of the local models and closed-form Procrustes updates of the edge-wise restriction maps. We further establish convergence of Sheaf-FRL to first-order stationary points in both deterministic and stochastic settings. As an application, we consider a cooperative classification task in the context of semantic communication, under model and data heterogeneity. Our results show that Sheaf-FRL outperforms baseline approaches in terms of local and post-communication classification accuracy across different levels of local distribution shift and exhibits greater robustness to latent-space dimensionality compression.

</details>


### [261] [DOCSCHISEL: Adaptive Tool Documentation Optimization Framework for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10037)
*You Lu,Kun Zhang,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体使用外部工具时，工具文档如何影响任务表现进行了大规模实证研究，发现不同信息字段的效果高度依赖任务领域、模型骨干和智能体范式。基于此，作者提出DocsChisel框架，通过分析失败执行轨迹自适应地优化工具文档，在任务成功率上较原始文档提升95.89%，较现有基线平均提升75.15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注提升LLM智能体的工具使用能力，而将工具文档视为固定输入。尽管有少数工作尝试通过重写或压缩优化工具文档，但对工具文档中包含的信息如何在不同设置下影响智能体表现知之甚少。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 首先进行大规模实证研究，揭示现有工具文档在信息字段上存在显著异质性，且不同信息字段的有效性高度依赖任务领域、LLM骨干和智能体范式。基于此提出DocsChisel框架：通过分析目标LLM智能体的失败执行轨迹来识别文档相关问题，然后针对每个工具迭代地添加、删除和优化信息字段，实现工具文档的自适应优化。

Result: 在任务成功率上，DocsChisel相比原始工具文档提升95.89%，相比现有最优基线（EasyTool和DRAFT）平均提升75.15%，同时优化时间和token开销均保持在较低水平。

Conclusion: 工具文档不存在"一刀切"的最优方案，其信息字段的有效性随任务领域、模型和智能体范式而变化。DocsChisel通过自适应优化框架，能够有效提升LLM智能体在不同设置下的任务成功率，为工具文档的自动化优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on external tools to accomplish complex real-world tasks, making tool documentation a critical grounding resource for LLM agents. Existing studies mainly focus on improving the tool-use capabilities of LLM agents, while largely treating tool documentation as a fixed input. Although several recent works attempt to optimize tool documentation through rewriting or compression, little is known about how the information contained in tool documentation affects agent performance across different settings.
  To bridge this gap, we conduct a large-scale empirical study on tool documentation for LLM agents. Our study reveals substantial heterogeneity in the information fields provided by existing tool documentation. Moreover, the effectiveness of different information fields is highly dependent on the task domain, LLM backbone, and agent paradigm, indicating that no fixed tool documentation can consistently generalize across diverse agent settings.
  Motivated by these findings, we propose DocsChisel, an adaptive tool documentation optimization framework for LLM agents. DocsChisel analyzes failed execution traces of a target LLM agent to identify documentation-related issues, and iteratively optimizes tool documentation by adding, removing, and refining information fields for each tool. We evaluate DocsChisel against two state-of-the-art baselines, i.e., EasyTool and DRAFT. Experimental results show that DocsChisel improves the task success rate of LLM agents by 95.89% over the original tool documentation and by 75.15%, on average, over existing baselines, while incurring limited optimization time and token overhead

</details>


### [262] [FlowScout: From Execution Feedback to Reliable Tool-Using Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10039)
*Shuo Hao,You Lu,Bihuan Chen,Xin Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowScout 是一种从历史任务解决记录中自动生成工具集成型智能体工作流的框架，通过挖掘工具协调骨架并结合蒙特卡洛树搜索与执行反馈来优化工作流拓扑，在工具调用正确性和执行质量上显著超越现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体工作流的构建高度依赖人工和领域专家知识，现有自动生成方法（如基于历史记录的方法）主要产生以LLM为中心的工作流，其中真实工具执行被LLM节点抽象和模拟，导致生成的工作流可用性和稳定性受限。因此需要一种能生成真正工具集成型工作流的方法。

Method: FlowScout 将智能体工作流建模为由LLM节点、工具调用节点和依赖边构成的有向图。方法分两步：(1) 从历史任务解决记录中挖掘通用的工具协调骨架，构建初始工作流；(2) 以执行反馈为导向，通过蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）对工作流拓扑进行迭代优化和精炼。

Result: 在四个代表性任务领域上与三个基线方法（PM4Py、ReAct、AFlow）对比，FlowScout 生成的智能体工作流在工具调用正确性上至少提升 92.69%，执行质量至少提升 17.66%，且在多次重复运行中表现出更低的性能方差。

Conclusion: FlowScout 通过执行引导的框架有效解决了工具集成型工作流自动生成的问题，生成的稀疏工具集成工作流在可靠性和稳定性方面均优于现有方法，为自动化智能体工作流构建提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Agentic workflows have become an important abstraction for building reliable LLM-based automation systems by organizing large language models (LLMs), tools, and control logic into explicit execution structures. However, constructing high-quality agentic workflows remains largely manual and requires substantial domain expertise. Recent studies have explored automatic agentic workflow generation from historical task-solving records, but they mainly produce LLM-centric workflows, where real tool executions are abstracted and simulated by LLM nodes, limiting the usability and stability of generated workflows. To address these limitations, we propose FlowScout, an execution-guided framework for generating tool-integrated agentic workflows from historical task-solving records. Specifically, FlowScout represents an agentic workflow as a directed graph composed of LLM nodes, tool-calling nodes, and dependency edges. It first mines a common tool coordination skeleton from historical records to construct an initial workflow, and then refines the workflow topology through Monte Carlo tree search guided by execution feedback. We evaluate FlowScout on four representative task domains and compare it with three baselines, i.e., PM4Py, ReAct and AFlow. Experimental results show that agentic workflows generated by FlowScout improve tool invocation correctness by at least 92.69% and execution quality by at least 17.66% over the baselines, while achieving lower performance variation across repeated runs.

</details>


### [263] [UserToolBench: A User-Profile-Hidden Benchmark for Personalized Decision Making in Tool-Use LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10042)
*Xuexiong Yin,Zechuan Chen,Yongsen Zheng,Yuxiang Zhang,Jingyuan Yang,Bin Wang,Yubin Wang,Keze Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UserToolBench 是一个用于评估工具型 LLM 个性化决策能力的基准，包含 10 个用户画像、36 个工具集、1065 轮对话和 170 个独特工具。实验表明，当前模型在个性化委托方面仍存在困难，多工具协调、缺失约束推理和长期行为一致性是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具型 LLM 基准测试多关注画像回忆、风格模仿、通用工具使用或响应级个性化，缺乏对模型是否能为用户做出正确决策的评估。作者旨在填补这一空白，推动个性化评估从"输出听起来是否像用户"转向"决策是否真正符合用户利益"。

Method: 构建 UserToolBench 基准：基于隐私脱敏的真实交互轨迹，结合结构化用户画像（10 个）、公共 API 风格工具生态系统（36 个工具集、170 个独特工具）、长程多轮轨迹（1065 轮），并设计三种评估任务类型——信息缺失、单工具和多工具场景，测试模型从交互历史推断潜在用户偏好、识别需澄清的时机以及在信息不完整条件下生成用户对齐的工具调用轨迹的能力。

Result: 在强工具型 LLM 上的实验表明：当前模型在个性化委托方面表现不佳；多工具协同、缺失约束推理和长程行为一致性是三个主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 个性化评估应超越"输出是否听起来用户特定"的浅层标准，转向评估 LLM 是否真正为用户做出正确决策。UserToolBench 为此提供了系统性测试平台。

Abstract: Tool-use LLMs are increasingly asked to act on users' behalf, but existing benchmarks usually focus on profile recall, style imitation, generic tool use, or response-level personalization. We introduce UserToolBench , a benchmark for personalized decision making in tool-use LLMs. UserToolBench tests whether a model can infer latent user preferences from interaction history, recognize when clarification is needed, and produce user-aligned tool-call trajectories under incomplete information. The benchmark is built from privacy-sanitized real interaction traces and combines structured persona profiles, public API-style tool ecosystems, and long-horizon multi-turn trajectories. It includes 10 user profiles, 36 tool sets, 1,065 turns, 170 unique tools, and evaluation-focused task types covering lack-of-information, single-tool, and multi-tool settings. Experiments with strong tool-use LLMs show that current models still have difficulty with personalized delegation. Multi-tool coordination, missing-constraint inference, and long-horizon behavioral consistency remain major bottlenecks. These results suggest that personalization evaluation should move beyond asking whether outputs sound user-specific and instead ask whether LLMs make correct decisions for the users they represent.

</details>


### [264] [Finding the Signal in the Spam: Jointly Learning Rewards and Worker Reliability from Pairwise Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10045)
*Kaustubh Shivshankar Shejole,Tanish Agarwal,Arpit Agarwal,Avishek Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在众包场景下从成对比较中联合学习物品奖励和工人可靠性（胜任力）的问题。作者在Bradley-Terry-Luce模型基础上引入工人胜任力参数，提出基于EM算法的学习方法，利用Polya-Gamma潜变量将logistic似然转化为条件高斯形式，使优化可处理并简化为矩阵感知问题，从而建立理论收敛保证。实验表明该方法在真实和合成数据上均优于多个基线，对垃圾发送者和对抗性工人具有强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包平台（如MTurk、Scale AI）上的工人常因领域知识有限或逐利行为（垃圾发送）而不可靠，现有成对比较学习方法多假设工人同质且可靠。作者希望探究能否在估计物品奖励的同时，联合学习每个工人的胜任力（可靠性），以提升奖励学习的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: 采用Boltzmann-rational模型（Bradley-Terry-Luce的扩展，引入工人胜任力参数）。核心技术创新：引入Polya-Gamma潜变量，将logistic似然函数转化为条件高斯形式，使EM算法的E步得到简化的Q函数，进而将问题规约为矩阵感知（matrix sensing）问题，并据此建立理论收敛保证。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的大量实验表明：（1）所提算法在奖励估计和工人胜任力估计上均优于多个基线方法；（2）对垃圾发送者和对抗性工人均表现出强鲁棒性；（3）验证了该方法在真实众包和奖励学习场景中的实用有效性。

Conclusion: 联合学习工人可靠性与物品奖励是可行且有效的。基于Polya-Gamma增强的EM算法能够以理论可保证的方式解决该问题，在众包成对比较场景中具有显著的实际应用价值。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: The problem of learning from pairwise comparisons has been widely studied across many domains such as recommendation systems, social choice, and more recently, fine-tuning large language models. In this problem, the goal is to learn item rewards based on pairwise comparisons between them. In many scenarios, these comparisons are elicited from crowdworkers using platforms such as Amazon Mechanical Turk, Scale AI, etc. However, crowdworkers are often unreliable due to limited domain knowledge or revenue-maximizing (spamming) behavior. In this work, our goal is to understand whether worker reliability (competency) can be learned jointly with item rewards. To this end, we adopt the Boltzmann-rational model for pairwise comparisons, which extends the Bradley-Terry-Luce model by incorporating worker competencies. We derive an EM-based algorithm for learning under this model by introducing Polya-Gamma latent variables to transform the logistic likelihood into a conditionally Gaussian form, enabling tractable optimization and leading to a simplified $Q$ function in the E-step of the algorithm. This technique allows us to reduce our formulation to a matrix sensing problem, using which we establish theoretical convergence guarantees for our algorithm. We conduct extensive experiments on real-world and synthetic datasets. These experiments demonstrate the advantages of using our algorithm over several baselines and confirm its strong robustness to both spammers and adversarial workers, highlighting its practical effectiveness in realistic crowdsourcing and reward learning settings. The code and data is publicly available at https://github.com/KaustubhShejole/BoRa_EM.

</details>


### [265] [Path Integral Value Matching for Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10777)
*Bangyan Liao,Chenglei Yu,Yuchen Yang,Chuanrui Wang,Zhisheng Song,Peidong Liu,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于价值函数的路径积分价值匹配（PI-VM）算法，通过截断和边缘化路径积分形式推导出值函数的时序递归形式，结合时序差分学习与Girsanov定理实现离线训练，在保持精度的同时大幅提升计算效率并缓解高维模式坍塌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于策略的LQ-SOC方法依赖全轨迹仿真，计算成本高且不稳定；标准路径积分控制（PIC）同样面临高方差瓶颈。需要一种更高效、稳定的替代方案。

Method: 对路径积分控制进行截断与边缘化，推导出值函数的时序递归形式；提出PI-VM算法，使用时序差分（TD）学习近似递归值动态，结合Girsanov定理与经验回放实现离线策略（off-policy）训练。

Result: 在多种SOC基准与采样任务上，PI-VM在低维场景中以一个数量级的效率增益达到SOTA精度；在高维场景中有效缓解模式坍塌问题。

Conclusion: PI-VM为复杂随机最优控制问题提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，标志着从基于策略到基于价值的方法范式转变。

Abstract: Linear Quadratic Stochastic Optimal Control (LQ-SOC) establishes a fundamental framework for steering noisy dynamical systems and has recently gained renewed interest in the machine learning community. However, current state-of-the-art policy-based methods suffer from prohibitive computational costs and instability due to their heavy reliance on full-trajectory simulation. To overcome these limitations, we propose a paradigm shift toward a value-based approach by revisiting Path Integral Control (PIC). Although standard PIC suffers from the same high-variance bottleneck as policy-based methods, we discover that by truncating and marginalizing the original path integral formulation, we can derive a temporal recursive form of the value function. Building upon this theoretical foundation, we propose the Path Integral Value Matching (PI-VM) algorithm. Specifically, we employ temporal-difference learning to approximate the recursive value dynamics, and further integrate the Girsanov theorem with experience replay to enable off-policy training. We benchmark PI-VM against SOTA policy-based methods across various SOC benchmarks and sampling tasks. Empirical results demonstrate that PI-VM matches SOTA precision with an order-of-magnitude efficiency gain in low-dimensional settings, while effectively mitigating mode collapse in high-dimensional scenarios. Consequently, PI-VM offers a scalable solution for solving complex SOC problems.

</details>


### [266] [Detecting Soft Skills in ML Engineering Roles CVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10046)
*Aidin Azamnouri,Nouran Ayad,Justus Bogner,Stefan Wagner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过LLM管道从300份ML工程师、数据科学家和软件工程师的简历中提取显式与隐式软技能，将需求方文献的主张转化为13个可检验假设，发现候选人主要通过叙事（而非关键词列表）披露软技能，比例为3:1；领导力、协调和指导能力几乎完全以叙事形式呈现；软件工程师表达领导力的概率仅为同行一半。关键词筛选会系统性地遗漏这些能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对ML系统开发中软技能的研究几乎全部来自需求方（招聘广告、调查、招聘经理访谈），缺乏候选人视角。已有的简历挖掘工作存在两个关键缺陷：一是仅基于关键词，无法捕捉通过叙事传达的软技能；二是仅做描述性频率排名，未检验组间差异是否超出抽样误差。本研究旨在填补这两方面空白。

Method: 构建三个角色（ML工程师、数据科学家、软件工程师）各100份的平衡语料库（共300份经筛选的简历）。开发基于LLM的提取管道，区分"显式列出"与"隐式叙事"两类软技能披露方式，并通过人工标注的真值进行验证。将需求方文献的主张转化为13个可证伪假设，涵盖角色特征、资历进阶和披露风格三个维度，采用效应量和族系误差控制进行假设检验。

Result: 13个假设中11个得到支持，1个部分支持，1个被反驳。候选人通过叙事披露软技能的比例约为关键词列表的3倍（约3:1）。雇主最看重的领导力、协调能力和指导能力几乎完全以叙事形式呈现（88-96%为叙事）。资历每提升一级，表达领导力的几率增加近三倍。软件工程师表达领导力的比率仅为同行的50%，挑战了先前研究认为该能力具有普遍性的假设。

Conclusion: 技术人员确实会在简历中表达软技能，但基于关键词的筛选方法会系统性地遗漏这些信息。研究为候选人视角的软技能研究提供了方法基础和实证证据，对招聘实践和简历自动筛选系统的设计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Soft skills shape collaboration among ML engineers, data scientists, and software engineers building ML-enabled systems, yet what we know about them comes almost entirely from the demand side. Job advertisements, surveys, and hiring manager interviews capture what employers ask for. How candidates themselves articulate these competencies has not been studied, and existing CV-mining work is both keyword-based, so it cannot see skills conveyed through narrative, and descriptive, reporting frequency rankings without testing whether group differences exceed sampling variation. We close both gaps. Using a balanced corpus of 300 curated CVs spanning the three roles, we extract explicitly listed and implicitly narrated soft skills with an LLM-based pipeline validated against a human-annotated ground truth, a distinction that existing extractors were not designed to make. We then convert the demand-side literature's claims into 13 falsifiable hypotheses about role signatures, seniority progression, and disclosure style, and test them with effect sizes under family-wise error control, so that candidate-side data can corroborate or contradict the demand-side account rather than merely illustrate it. Eleven hypotheses are supported, one partially, and one refuted. Candidates disclose soft skills through narrative rather than keyword lists by roughly three to one, and most so for the competencies employers value most: leadership, coordination, and mentoring (88-96% narrative). Seniority nearly triples the odds of articulating leadership. That competency, assumed universal in prior work, is articulated by software engineers at half the rate of their peers. Technical candidates do articulate soft skills, but a keyword-based screening systematically misses them.

</details>


### [267] [Optimistic Rates for Multiclass PAC Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10869)
*Xiaoyu Li,Andi Han,Jiaojiao Jiang,Junbin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对多分类问题，在任意固定 oracle 风险 L* 下，给出了最优超额风险的紧刻画：Θ̃(√(L* d_N/n) + d_DS/n)，统一了可实现与不可知两端点之间的开放区域，并将结果扩展至列表学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多分类最坏情况界的缺陷在于：当最佳分类器已接近正确时，界不会变小。亟需一种"乐观速率"——保证的波动随 oracle 风险本身缩放。此前，Natarajan 维度 d_N 和 Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz 维度 d_DS 的最优超额风险仅在两端点已知，中间区域是开放的。

Method: 上界：将 CEH+26 的 cover-menu-compression 架构与 Pab26 的可实现速率结合，并提出新的"比较器面向的相对压缩定理"——一个经验上优于比较器 h 的大小为 k 的压缩规则，其总体风险至多为 L(h)+O(√(L(h)Γ)+Γ)，其中 Γ=(k log n+log(1/δ))/n，无需稳定性。该定理将 MQZ26 的尖锐二分类比较原理迁移至多分类，同时舍弃了无法提升到多标签的布尔立方几何。下界：通过针对 L* 校准的 pair-Assouad 方案，以及在 BCD+22 的 Natarajan-vs-DS 分离伪立方上的纤维论证，用单一类别和单一分布在每个固定 L* 处同时强制两项。

Result: 在任意固定 oracle 风险 L* 下，最优超额风险为 Θ̃(√(L* d_N/n) + d_DS/n)，对字母表大小一致成立，且由无需知晓 L* 或置信水平的学习者实现。结果扩展至列表学习：针对最佳 r 元假设组，相同架构和引擎给出相同形状的乐观速率和下界，强制了 Pab26 预期的波动项，并移除了已知可实现列表下界中的因子 r。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了多分类中从可实现到不可知全谱系的乐观速率问题，揭示了风险波动由 √(L* d_N/n) 主导，并由 d_DS/n 的全局偏移补充。技术上，新的相对压缩定理实现了无稳定性的比较迁移，pair-Assouad 下界方法精准匹配了两项，两者均无缝泛化至列表学习场景。

Abstract: Worst-case multiclass bounds do not become smaller when the best classifier is already nearly correct: what is missing is an optimistic rate, a guarantee whose fluctuation scales with the oracle risk itself. For a class of Natarajan dimension $d_N$ and Daniely-Shalev-Shwartz dimension $d_{DS}$, the optimal excess risk is known at the two endpoints ($d_{DS}/n$ realizable, $\sqrt{d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n$ agnostic [HMZ24, CEH+26, Pab26]) and open in between. We close the gap: at every fixed oracle risk $L^\star$, the optimal excess risk is $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{L^\star d_N/n}+d_{DS}/n)$, uniformly in the alphabet size, attained by a learner that knows neither $L^\star$ nor the confidence level. The upper bound composes the cover-menu-compression architecture of [CEH+26], at the realizable rate of [Pab26], with a new comparator-facing relative compression theorem: a size-$k$ compression rule that empirically dominates a comparator $h$ has population risk at most $L(h)+O(\sqrt{L(h)Γ}+Γ)$ with $Γ=(k\log n+\log(1/δ))/n$, without stability; this transfers the comparison principle of the sharp binary theory [MQZ26] while discarding its Boolean-cube geometry, which does not lift to multiclass labels. The lower bound forces both terms using one class and one distribution at every fixed $L^\star$, by a pair-Assouad scheme calibrated to $L^\star$ and a fiber argument on the pseudo-cubes underlying the Natarajan-versus-DS separation of [BCD+22]. Both theorems extend to list learning: against the best $r$-tuple of hypotheses, the same architecture and the same two engines yield an optimistic rate and a lower bound of the same shape, forcing the fluctuation term that [Pab26] expected to be necessary against list comparators, and removing the factor $r$ from the known realizable list lower bound.

</details>


### [268] [Physics-Informed Machine Learning in Prognostics and Health Management: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10047)
*Christopher Braun,Julian Raible,Marco F. Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了物理信息机器学习（PIML）在PHM中的应用，提出四类分类方案（观测偏置、归纳偏置、学习偏置、混合方法），发现PIML在预测性能上普遍优于纯数据驱动基线，但文献过度集中于锂电池和轴承，且对可解释性、因果推理等方面的改进缺乏充分证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的机器学习模型在PHM中存在泛化能力差、无法推断因果关系、缺乏可解释性等固有局限。PIML通过将先验物理知识融入ML流程有望缓解这些问题，但该领域缺乏系统性梳理与分类框架。

Method: 对212篇文献进行系统综述；提出四类PIML分类方案：观测偏置（数据增强/预处理中引入物理知识）、归纳偏置（模型架构设计融入物理约束）、学习偏置（损失函数/优化目标中嵌入物理规律）、混合方法（组合上述多种策略）；并按PHM任务（诊断、预测等）进一步归类。

Result: 四类方法在多种资产上均一致展现出优于传统纯数据驱动基线的预测性能；但文献严重偏向锂电池和轴承两类对象，且以针对特定问题的定制化解决方案为主；关于可解释性、因果推理等局限性的改进声称缺乏充分实证。

Conclusion: PIML在PHM中已带来切实性能收益，但未来研究需关注可迁移的设计模式、不同融合策略的基准对比，以及适用于真实在线部署的轻量鲁棒不确定性感知模型。

Abstract: In modern industry, keeping complex systems reliable, safe, and efficient hinges on Prognostics and Health Management (PHM). Machine Learning (ML) has largely driven advancements in diagnostics and prognostics, yet purely data-driven models face inherent limitations, such as poor generalization, an inability to infer causal relationships, and a lack of interpretability. Physics-Informed Machine Learning (PIML) helps mitigate these limitations by incorporating prior physical knowledge directly into the ML pipeline, thereby fostering growing interest in its application to PHM. This work investigates how PIML is being leveraged in the context of PHM through a systematic literature review of 212 studies. The review introduces a four-class classification scheme, consisting of observational bias, inductive bias, learning bias, and hybrid approaches, and further categorizes studies by PHM task. Across all four classes, the reviewed studies consistently demonstrate improved predictive performance over conventional baselines across a broad range of assets, although the literature is heavily skewed toward lithium-ion batteries and bearings, and dominated by problem-specific solutions. Overall, the review indicates that physics-informed approaches already provide tangible benefits, whereas claims of improvements concerning some of the aforementioned limitations lack sufficient supporting evidence. Future research should prioritize transferable design patterns, benchmarks comparing integration strategies, and uncertainty-aware models that are lightweight and robust enough for online deployment in real-world settings.

</details>


### [269] [Efficient Hypergradient Descent for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11052)
*Nikita Sevriukov,Anna Barabanova,Uliana Gagarina,Karina Ivanova,Sofiia Kasaeva,Ilya Levin,Marina Sheshukova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对逆强化学习（IRL）的双层优化框架，揭示了在内层最优处Hessian矩阵与策略Fisher信息矩阵之间的比例关系，从而导出结构化的Fisher超梯度；并采用流式频谱草图近似逆Fisher-向量积，避免显式构建大规模Fisher矩阵，在保持策略性能和奖励排序质量的同时，降低了曲率存储复杂度并提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IRL的双层优化形式虽然自然，但外层的超梯度计算需要内层目标函数的逆Hessian-向量积，在实践中的计算代价极高。现有方法要么回避这一难点，要么面临大规模Fisher矩阵带来的可扩展性瓶颈。本文旨在利用内层最优处的特殊结构来简化超梯度计算，并解决由此引入的Fisher矩阵存储与求逆的扩展性问题。

Method: 首先，作者推导出在内层策略最优处，内层目标的Hessian与策略的Fisher信息矩阵成比例，从而将超梯度转化为结构化的"Fisher超梯度"，与自然超梯度下降密切相关。其次，为应对大规模Fisher矩阵的扩展性瓶颈，采用流式频谱草图（streaming spectral sketch）来近似逆Fisher-向量积，避免显式构造和存储完整的Fisher矩阵。

Result: 在离散和连续控制环境下的实验表明：所提方法在策略性能上具有竞争力，在奖励排序质量上表现强劲；同时，Fisher草图方法相较于显式Fisher求解器，降低了曲率存储复杂度，并在计算效率上有所提升。

Conclusion: 通过揭示内层最优处的Hessian-Fisher比例关系，IRL的超梯度计算可被显著简化；结合流式频谱草图技术，可在不牺牲性能的前提下有效突破大规模Fisher矩阵带来的可扩展性与存储瓶颈。

Abstract: Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) aims to recover a reward function under which the resulting policy reproduces the behavior observed in expert demonstrations. A natural approach is to formulate IRL as a bilevel optimization problem, in which the inner level corresponds to policy optimization under the learned reward and the outer level measures the discrepancy between the induced policy and expert data. However, this formulation is computationally challenging in practice because the outer update requires a hypergradient involving an inverse-Hessian-vector product for the inner objective. We address this challenge by showing that, at the inner optimum, the Hessian of the inner objective is proportional to the Fisher information matrix of the policy, yielding a structured Fisher-based hypergradient closely related to Natural Hypergradient Descent. To address the resulting scalability bottleneck associated with large Fisher matrices, we approximate the required inverse-Fisher-vector product using a streaming spectral sketch, avoiding explicit construction of the Fisher matrix. We evaluate our approach against a first-order stochastic bilevel baseline across discrete- and continuous-control environments. The results demonstrate competitive policy performance and strong reward-ranking quality, while Fisher sketching reduces curvature-storage complexity and can improve computational efficiency relative to an explicit Fisher solver.

</details>


### [270] [Observational Policy Ranking for SMB Financial Guidance from Multi-Action Accounting Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10050)
*Shrutendra Harsola,Vignesh Subrahmaniam,Vikas Raturi,Kamalika Das,Xiang Gao,Kratika Gupta,Ruocheng Guo,Padmaja Jonnalagedda,Ananya Pramod,Sricharan Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAR-PL（协变量调整残差策略学习），一种针对多热编码会计日志的观测性策略排序方法，用于从85,078条公司-月份观测中为中小企业选择最优财务指导类别。在独立留存企业上，CAR-PL在毛利KPI上表现最佳，且选择分布更均匀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业需要及时财务指导，但历史会计日志记录的是企业自选的、经常共现的业务变更，而非随机化推荐，传统方法难以从这类观测数据中有效学习最优策略。因此需要一种能够在多动作、观测性设置下进行个性化策略排序的方法。

Method: 提出CAR-PL（Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning），一种基于动作层面R-learner的方法，直接处理多热编码日志，并通过观测支持度进行选择正则化。在7,505家企业的85,078条公司-月份数据上，从34个账本衍生的业务变更类别中为三个财务KPI（毛利、收入、速动比率）选择策略。比较对象包括uplift T-Learner、保守型上下文价值模型、零样本LLM及非个性化基线，使用公司不相交的留存样本和模型辅助评分规则评估。

Result: CAR-PL在毛利KPI上点估计最高（0.084）；T-Learner在收入上最高（0.085）；上下文价值模型在速动比率上最高（0.062）。CAR-PL与T-Learner在两个增长KPI上经公司聚类匹配比较无统计显著差异。CAR-PL选择33-34个类别，选择分布更均匀。纯结果模型评分保留相同KPI层面的点估计领先者或前两名，类别排序在将全零处理参考替换为最常见训练共现模式后保持相似。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，可以从多动作会计日志中进行针对特定目标的SMB财务指导排序，CAR-PL在毛利优化上具有优势，且在不同评分方法下排名稳健，支持观测性策略排序在中小企业财务指导中的实际应用。

Abstract: Small and medium-sized businesses need timely financial guidance, yet historical accounting logs record self-selected and often co-occurring business changes rather than randomized recommendations. We formulate this setting as observational policy ranking: from pre-decision financial information, a policy selects one of 34 ledger-derived business-change categories for a target financial KPI. Using 85,078 company-month observations from 7,505 firms, we introduce Covariate-Adjusted Residual Policy Learning (CAR-PL), an action-wise R-learner that operates directly on multi-hot logs and regularizes selection by observational support. We compare CAR-PL with an uplift T-Learner, a conservative contextual value model, a zero-shot LLM, and non-personalized references on company-disjoint held-out firms under a shared model-assisted scoring rule. CAR-PL has the highest Gross Profit point estimate (0.084), the T-Learner has the highest Revenue point estimate (0.085), and the contextual value model has the highest Quick Ratio point estimate (0.062). CAR-PL and the T-Learner are not statistically separated on either growth KPI in matched company-clustered comparisons, while CAR-PL selects 33-34 categories and produces less concentrated selections across the catalog. Outcome-model-only scoring retains the same KPI-level point-estimate leader or top pair, and category rankings remain similar when the all-zero treatment reference is replaced by the most common training co-action pattern. These findings support objective-specific ranking of SMB financial guidance from multi-action accounting logs.

</details>


### [271] [ChronoSSM: Training for Temporally Aware Representations in Autoregressive State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10120)
*Adrien Schoen,Nachiketa Ratnakar Patil,Arjun Bhagoji,Francesco Bronzino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ChronoSSM 是一种自回归状态空间模型，通过共享骨干网络联合建模事件与时间戳，在保持内容生成质量的同时，使学到的表征包含更丰富的时间结构信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列模型（如 Transformer、SSM）通常将"预测发生什么"作为主任务，而将"何时发生"作为辅助信号，采用两阶段方法（先用事件预测训练表征，再冻结表征训练时间模型）。这种做法隐含假设事件预测优化的表征已包含足够的时间结构，但这一假设未必成立。本文旨在验证联合训练能否在不损害事件建模质量的前提下，产生更具时间信息量的表征。

Method: 提出 ChronoSSM，一种自回归状态空间模型，采用共享骨干网络，联合优化 token 生成和时间生成两个目标。在四个领域数据集上（覆盖稠密和部分时间戳监督），对比联合训练（时间监督更新骨干网络）与两阶段训练（仅在冻结的事件表征上学习时间信息）两种范式。

Result: 联合训练在所有四个领域上持续使冻结表征中的到达间隔信息更可恢复，且整体内容生成质量未出现系统性下降。表明时间监督可以产生更具时间信息量的表征，同时不会实质性地损害自回归事件建模性能。

Conclusion: 将事件与时间戳联合建模是可行的，且优于两阶段分离策略。时间监督不仅不损害内容生成，还能显著增强表征中的时间结构，为需要时序推理和异常检测的数据挖掘场景提供了更优的建模方案。
