Motivation: 传统朴素贝叶斯分类器的平滑规则（Laplace、Lidstone等）采用固定平滑强度，忽略了特征基数、样本量和类别不平衡等因素，在现代高基数表格数据上产生不可忽略的偏差。需要一种能够根据数据特性自适应调整平滑强度的方法。

Method: 提出HEB-NB：为每个类别-特征条件概率引入狄利克雷先验，其浓度参数通过Type-II最大似然（经验贝叶斯）进行数据自适应学习，实现跨类的信息共享且保持闭式推断。进一步将自适应平滑扩展到HEB-AODE（平均单依赖估计器）。理论分析包括非渐近ℓ₁误差界（匹配经验分布极小极大速率加上可消失的数据自适应偏差）、Laplace紧下界（证明有限样本下与Laplace的严格风险分离）、基于全变差张量化的插件超额贝叶斯风险界，以及总体Top-1期望校准误差推论。

Result: 在31个UCI和OpenML基准数据集上，HEB-NB在概率度量上获得最佳平均Friedman排名；在高基数数据集上对数损失降低高达22.1%；HEB-AODE相比普通AODE持续提升。结合互信息加权后，Top-1期望校准误差降低41%-70%。

Conclusion: HEB-NB通过数据自适应的层次经验贝叶斯平滑，有效克服了传统固定平滑规则的缺陷，在概率精度和校准性上均取得显著提升，且理论上有严格保证，具有良好的扩展性（如HEB-AODE）。

Abstract: The Naive Bayes (NB) classifier remains a standard choice for categorical data, yet its widely used smoothing rules, such as Laplace, Lidstone, Krichevsky-Trofimov, and the $m$-estimate, all prescribe a fixed smoothing strength that ignores feature cardinality, sample size, and class imbalance, inducing a non-vanishing bias on modern high-cardinality tabular data. We propose hierarchical empirical-Bayes Naive Bayes (HEB-NB), in which each class-feature conditional probability is smoothed by a Dirichlet prior whose concentration is learned data-adaptively via Type-II maximum likelihood, enabling principled information sharing across classes while retaining closed-form inference. We further introduce HEB average one-dependence estimators (HEB-AODE), showing that the adaptive smoothing transfers cleanly to structural relaxations of NB. Theoretically, we establish a non-asymptotic $\ell_1$ error bound for HEB-NB matching the empirical-distribution minimax rate plus a vanishing data-adaptive bias, together with a matching Laplace-tight lower bound that yields a finite-sample, risk-level strict separation from Laplace. We further derive a plug-in excess Bayes-risk bound via total-variation tensorization and a population top-1 expected calibration error (ECE) corollary. Empirically, across 31 UCI and OpenML benchmarks, HEB-NB attains the best average Friedman rank on probabilistic metrics, with up to 22.1% log-loss reductions on high-cardinality datasets and consistent improvements of HEB-AODE over vanilla AODE. Combining HEB-NB with mutual-information weighting reduces top-1 ECE by 41%-70%, demonstrating substantial gains in probabilistic accuracy and calibration.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [340] [Covariate Informed Identification of Heterogeneity and Outliers in Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10095)
*Anish Mukherjee,Jeremy T. Gaskins*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯混合效应模型，通过二值异质性指标和逻辑回归协变量建模，识别纵向数据中三类异质性：不兼容的均值轨迹、增大的残差方差和个体测量值的离群点，从而避免标准混合模型对残差方差的过高估计，实现更高效的固定效应参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向数据中常存在异质性，即某些个体的均值和方差与其他个体显著不同，部分个体还可能在少数测量点上出现离群值。标准的同方差混合效应模型会因此高估残差方差，导致估计效率低下。现有方法未能同时处理这三类异质性来源。

Method: 构建贝叶斯混合模型，引入三类二值异质性指标分别对应：不兼容的均值轨迹、增大的残差方差和个体测量值离群点。通过逻辑回归将协变量纳入异质性指标的建模。使用马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗（MCMC）进行统计推断，并通过模型选择评估各异质性成分的纳入必要性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提模型能准确识别纵向数据中的异质性，并产生高效的固定效应参数估计。在SWAN研究的DHEAS激素数据上进一步验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯混合模型能够有效识别并建模纵向数据中的三类异质性来源，相比标准混合效应模型，在参数估计效率上具有显著优势，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: We often observe heterogeneity in longitudinal data, where the mean and variance for certain profiles meaningfully differ from the rest. Some profiles may also exhibit outliers at a limited number of measurements. Using a standard mixed effects model, which assumes homogeneity, can lead to overestimating the residual variance and inefficient estimation. In this work, we identify and account for three sources of heterogeneity in longitudinal data: incompatible mean trajectories, increased residual variance, and outliers at individual measurements. Our Bayesian mixture model incorporates binary indicators of heterogeneity for each of these features, modeled through logistic regression using covariates. We perform statistical inference using Markov chain Monte Carlo and implement model selection to evaluate the inclusion of various heterogeneous components. Simulations demonstrate that our model can accurately identify heterogeneity and produce efficient estimates of the fixed effects parameters. We further validate our approach using the DHEAS hormone data from the SWAN study.

</details>


### [341] [Asymptotics of Nonparametric Estimation under General Non-monotone MAR Missingness: A Nonparametric Maximum Likelihood Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10113)
*Yating Zou,Huimin Hu,Jeffrey Näf*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文利用筛极大似然理论，在非单调随机缺失（MAR）条件下建立了无需对缺失机制建模的通用收敛速率，并证明在Hölder类上完整数据密度可以以极小极大速率估计，缺失仅通过常数项影响，并可通过EM算法直接在缺失数据上实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺失数据在实证研究中普遍存在，现有方法（如多重插补、逆概率加权）的理论保证有限，尤其对于非单调MAR情形，现有保证往往局限于简化设置或依赖对缺失机制的强假设。作者旨在突破这些限制，提供更一般的理论结果。

Method: 采用筛极大似然（sieve maximum likelihood）框架，在仅假设MAR和自然正性条件下（不限制缺失模式配置、不对缺失机制建模）推导通用收敛速率；将该框架应用于密度估计问题，并采用EM算法在缺失数据上直接近似实现估计量。

Result: 在Hölder类上，完整数据密度可以以极小极大速率估计（至多差一个对数因子），且估计速率不受缺失影响，缺失仅通过常数项体现。模拟中，该方法在不同缺失程度下与使用完整数据的核密度估计性能相当。

Conclusion: 在非单调MAR下，筛极大似然方法无需对缺失机制建模即可实现最优密度估计速率，且EM算法的实际表现与完整数据核密度估计可比，显著拓展了缺失数据场景下的理论保证范围。

Abstract: Missing data constitute a pervasive challenge in empirical research. Consequently, there is an ever-growing number of methods designed to address this challenge, with multiple imputation and inverse probability weighting the dominant strategies. Despite this, theoretical guarantees remain limited, particularly in the challenging case of non-monotone missing at random (MAR). When guarantees exist, they are often confined to simplified settings such as missing completely at random, monotone or block-wise missingness, or rest on restrictive assumptions about the missingness mechanism. In this paper, we utilize the theory of sieve maximum likelihood to establish a general rate of convergence under MAR that requires no modeling of the missingness mechanism and no restriction on the configuration of missing patterns, beyond MAR itself and a natural positivity condition. Applying this result to density estimation, we show that the complete-data density can be estimated at the minimax rate over a Hölder class, up to a logarithmic factor, for any prescribed smoothness level. The missingness does not affect the rate and enters only through a constant. The estimator is approximated in practice by a simple expectation-maximization (EM) algorithm operating on the incomplete data directly. In simulations, it performs comparably to the kernel density estimator supplied with the complete data across a wide range of missingness levels.

</details>


### [342] [Bandable Cumulant Tensors: Optimal Estimation and Applications in Non-Gaussian Data Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10161)
*Runshi Tang,Anru R. Zhang,Yuefeng Han,Wei Biao Wu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对高维高阶累积量估计的维数灾难问题，本文提出了一类可带状化的累积量结构及相应的锥形样本估计器。该估计器仅需计算局部条目而不构建完整张量，在谱范数下达到极小极大最优速率，并将理论保证直接传递至自回归、滑动平均模型估计及传感器阵列源定位等下游任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶累积量能捕捉协方差遗漏的非高斯相依结构，但在高维情形下面临严重挑战：d阶累积量张量包含p^d个条目，且插件样本累积量在张量谱范数下通常连速率最优性都无法保证。现有方法难以同时应对计算复杂度和统计效率两大难题。

Method: 本文聚焦有序数据，假设高阶交互随远离张量主对角线而衰减，提出可带状化的累积量类（bandable cumulant class）。核心方法为锥形样本累积量估计器（tapered sample cumulant estimator），只计算带宽k内的O(p k^{d-1})个局部条目，无需构造完整张量。在指数型尾部条件下，建立了非渐近谱范数界，将锥形偏差与随机误差分离；并给出匹配的极小极大下界。对亚高斯观测，当样本量n ≫ (k + log p)^{d-1}且在oracle带宽k处，锥形估计器达到极小极大速率，环境维度仅通过log p进入。

Result: 理论层面：锥形估计器的上界与下界在主导偏差项和随机项上匹配，证明了极小极大最优性；局部化抑制了导致次优性的高阶波动，使得锥形化在此场景中比在协方差估计中发挥更强作用。应用层面：谱范数保证直接传递至下游任务——自回归模型中的累积量Yule-Walker估计、滑动平均模型中的最小距离估计、传感器阵列中的匹配滤波源定位，均获得插件式误差界。模拟实验验证了理论，RR间期、空气质量和Neuropixels记录的实数据分析展示了稳定性提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入可带状化的累积量结构并配合锥形估计策略，本文成功解决了高维高阶累积量估计中计算不可行与统计次优性的双重困境。该方法不仅在理论上达到极小极大最优，且其谱范数保证可无缝迁移至多种时序与信号处理任务，具有广泛的实用价值。

Abstract: Higher-order cumulants capture the non-Gaussian dependence that covariance misses, but they are hard to use in high dimensions. An order-$d$ cumulant tensor has $p^d$ entries, and the plug-in sample cumulant is generally not even rate-optimal under the tensor spectral norm. For ordered data, both difficulties admit one remedy: assuming that higher-order interactions decay away from the main tensor diagonal, we introduce a bandable cumulant class and a tapered sample cumulant estimator that computes only $O(pk^{d-1})$ local entries at bandwidth $k$ and never forms the full tensor. Under exponential-type tail conditions, we prove nonasymptotic spectral-norm bounds that separate tapering bias from stochastic error, and a minimax lower bound over the same class that matches the leading bias and stochastic terms of the upper bound; for sub-Gaussian observations, the tapered estimator attains the minimax rate whenever $n\gg(k+\log p)^{d-1}$ at the oracle bandwidth $k$, with the ambient dimension entering only through $\log p$. Localization also suppresses the higher-order fluctuations behind this suboptimality, so tapering plays a stronger role here than in bandable covariance estimation. The spectral-norm guarantee transfers directly to downstream tasks, yielding plug-in error bounds for cumulant Yule--Walker estimation in autoregressive models, minimum-distance estimation in moving-average models, and matched-filter source localization in sensor arrays. Simulations corroborate the theory, and real-data analyses of RR-interval, air-quality, and Neuropixels recordings illustrate the resulting stability gains.

</details>


### [343] [Joint return levels of maximum temperature and minimum relative humidity by combining copulas with an extreme value framework for bimodal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10252)
*Beatriz G da Cruz Albernaz,Cira E G Otiniano,Carolyne Soares de Brito,Fidel E C Morales,Enzo Porto Brasil*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文采用基于Copula的方法，对巴西首都巴西利亚最高气温与最低相对湿度的联合分布进行建模，计算其重现期。通过拟合边际分布和选择最优Copula族，捕捉了变量间的非对称依赖结构和多模态联合密度特征，最终采用条件方法确定二元重现水平，为区域环境风险监测和气候变化适应策略提供量化依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 巴西利亚频繁遭受极端气候事件（极端高温、异常低温、长期干旱及野火），对人口和生态系统造成直接影响。为深入理解这些事件的成因与影响，需要对高温与低湿共现的极端场景进行联合概率建模，以量化其发生频率。

Method: 首先采用面向复杂系统极值建模的新方法拟合最高气温与最低相对湿度的边际分布；随后利用Copula对两变量间的依赖结构进行建模，评估包括旋转版本在内的多种Copula族，通过信息准则选取最优模型，该模型能够捕捉依赖结构中的非对称性和尾部行为，并再现联合密度的多模态特征；最后采用条件方法确定二元重现水平。

Result: 所选Copula模型充分捕捉了变量间的非对称依赖结构与尾部行为，并成功再现了联合密度中的多模态模式，暗示可能存在多种气候状态。通过条件方法确定了高温与低湿组合极端场景的二元重现期。

Conclusion: 该分析框架能够有效量化高温-低湿复合极端事件的预期频率，为巴西利亚地区的环境风险监测、预防措施规划及气候变化适应策略的制定提供了有价值的科学信息。

Abstract: Climate change has become a growing concern, particularly in regions experiencing increasingly frequent extreme events. In Brasília, the capital of Brazil, significant shifts in climate patterns have drawn attention, including episodes of intense heat, unusually cold weather, and prolonged dry periods, often accompanied by wildfires. These phenomena directly affect the population and local ecosystems, requiring detailed analyses to understand their causes and effects. In this work, the return period for the joint distribution of maximum air temperature and minimum relative humidity in Brasília is determined using a copula-based approach. First, the dependence structure between the variables is modeled through a copula, while the marginal distributions are fitted using the novel methodology for modelling extreme values in complex systems. Different copula families, including rotated versions, were evaluated using information criteria, leading to the selection of a model that adequately captured the dependence structure, including asymmetry and tail behavior. The selected model also reproduced the multimodal pattern observed in the joint density, a feature consistent with the possible presence of multiple climate regimes. In the context of climate data modelling, the bivariate return level was determined using a conditional approach to assess the occurrence of extreme scenarios characterized by high temperatures combined with low relative humidity. This analysis makes it possible to quantify the expected frequency of such events, providing valuable information for environmental risk monitoring, the planning of preventive measures, and the development of climate change adaptation strategies in the region.

</details>


### [344] [Structured dispersion modelling for mortality data using a Conway--Maxwell--Poisson specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10261)
*Jackie Siaw Tze Wong,Emiliano A. Valdez*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Conway-Maxwell-Poisson（CMP）分布的灵活死亡率建模框架，允许离散度的类型和程度随年龄和时间变化，采用贝叶斯MCMC估计。利用英格兰和威尔士男性死亡数据进行实证分析，结果表明死亡率计数的变异性在不同年龄和时期存在显著差异，对长寿风险校准和年金产品定价具有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有死亡率模型通常假设固定的离散结构（如Poisson假设等离散），这一假设在实践中很少得到满足，可能导致不确定性校准失准和预测性能下降。因此，需要一种能够灵活捕捉死亡率数据中不同离散特征的统一框架。

Method: 采用Conway-Maxwell-Poisson（CMP）分布作为核心建模分布，该分布能够统一容纳欠离散、等离散和过离散三种情形；允许离散度的类型与程度随年龄和时间动态变化，以捕捉结构性异质性；采用贝叶斯推断框架，将离散参数视为未知量，通过合理的先验结构对参数不确定性、过程不确定性和分布不确定性进行一致性传播；利用马尔可夫链蒙特卡罗（MCMC）方法进行参数估计。

Result: 基于英格兰和威尔士男性死亡数据的实证分析表明，死亡率计数的变异性在不同年龄组和不同时期之间存在实质性差异，这一发现对长寿风险校准和年金产品定价具有显著意义。

Conclusion: 灵活考虑年龄和时间维度上的异质离散结构对于死亡率建模至关重要。所提出的CMP-Bayes框架能够更准确地刻画死亡计数的不确定性，从而改善长寿风险的量化管理和金融产品的精算定价。

Abstract: Mortality models that attempt to capture dispersion typically assume a fixed dispersion structure, an assumption that is rarely satisfied in practice and that can lead to miscalibrated uncertainty and poor predictive performance. In this paper, we introduce a flexible framework for explicitly modelling dispersion in mortality data using the Conway--Maxwell--Poisson (CMP) distribution, which accommodates underdispersion, equidispersion, and overdispersion within a unified specification. Rather than imposing a global dispersion parameter, the framework allows both the type and degree of dispersion to vary by age and over time, thus capturing structural heterogeneity that simpler models may overlook. A Bayesian formulation treats dispersion as unknown, with prior structures that coherently propagate parameter, process, and distributional uncertainty. Estimation is carried out via Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods. Using empirical death data for males in England and Wales, we show that variability in mortality counts differs substantially across ages and across time periods. This has meaningful implications for the calibration of longevity risk and the pricing of annuity products.

</details>


### [345] [Cohen's f or Mean Standardized Differences? Assessing Covariate Balance with Multivalued Treatments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10266)
*Ariel Linden*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文对多组（>2）处理情境下的协变量平衡评估进行了系统比较，将传统的成对标准化均值差（SMD）汇总方法与Cohen's f效应量框架进行对比。通过Stata命令esizereg扩展、模拟研究及理论推导，表明Cohen's f在追踪下游估计偏差方面与平均绝对SMD表现相当（r=0.93），而最大绝对SMD最弱；同时推导了f与成对SMD之间的精确函数关系，指出传统SMD阈值不可直接应用于f。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多处理组情境下的协变量平衡评估缺乏公认的omnibus标准。现行做法是平均或取最大成对SMD，而Cohen's f作为Cohen's d向多组的经典推广，提供了一个基于成熟效应量框架的替代方案，但两者从未被正式比较。

Method: （1）通过社区贡献的Stata命令esizereg将Cohen's f和SMD统计量扩展至任意加权和协变量调整模型；（2）在3、4、6个处理组的模拟研究中，分别在正确设定和错误设定的广义倾向评分加权下，将各统计量与下游处理效应偏差进行相关性分析；（3）在故意设计的非线性/交互结局模型下进行稳健性检验；（4）理论推导：给出f作为成对SMD的规模加权二次函数的精确表示，并证明等组权重下f与平均绝对SMD的最小可达比值。

Result: Cohen's f与合并平均绝对SMD在追踪估计偏差方面表现相当（r=0.93），在加权组内也类似（r≈0.79）；最大绝对SMD表现最弱。该排序在对抗性非线性/交互结局模型下保持不变。理论上，f与SMD统计量不可直接数值比较，且传统SMD阈值不应直接应用于f。

Conclusion: 推荐在多组协变量平衡评估中报告Cohen's f及其按水平分解和成对分解结果，以弥补传统仅依赖成对SMD汇总的不足。

Abstract: Assessing covariate balance across more than two treatment groups has no established omnibus standard: the prevailing practice averages, or takes the maximum of, pairwise standardized mean differences (SMD), while Cohen's f - the classical generalization of Cohen's d to more than two groups - offers an alternative grounded in an established effect-size framework, but the two have not been formally compared. We extend both to arbitrary weighted and covariate-adjusted models via a community-contributed Stata command, esizereg, and validate them in a simulation study of three, four, and six treatment groups under correctly specified and misspecified generalized-propensity-score weighting, correlating each statistic against downstream treatment-effect bias. Cohen's f tracks estimation bias comparably to mean absolute SMD, both pooled (r = 0.93) and within weighting arm (r approximately 0.79); maximum absolute SMD is generally weakest. This ranking was unchanged under a deliberately adversarial nonlinear/interaction outcome model. Cohen's f and the SMD-based statistics are not numerically comparable: we derive an exact representation of f as a size-weighted quadratic function of the pairwise SMDs and prove the minimum attainable ratio between f and mean absolute SMD under equal group weights, so conventional SMD thresholds should not apply directly to f. We recommend reporting f alongside its per-level and pairwise decomposition.

</details>


### [346] [COMPACT: Spectral Adjustment Scores from a Complete and Irreducible Causal Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10305)
*Eric V. Strobl*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的倾向性评分方法，通过广义特征值问题在平衡性坐标与结局导向坐标的张成空间中构建评分，无需直接观测全部混杂变量即可在存在隐变量的因果图中实现最大特异性调整，并通过理论和实验验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观测性研究中，研究者常面临高维基线变量却不知哪些应纳入调整集；混杂信息可能分散于众多变量中。传统倾向性评分虽是最粗糙的平衡评分，但分布最优性不等同于因果图上的最大特异性。现有方法未充分考虑隐变量下因果图结构的特异性表达。

Method: 作者在所有包含候选评分、处理和结局的因果图中，允许隐变量存在，基于忠实性假设识别在"处理是否导致结局"这一条件不变下成立的最大无条件及条件依赖关系集，定义最大特异性图类。随后通过广义特征值问题将该准则操作化，其评分空间同时瞄准平衡坐标和结局导向坐标的张成空间，并证明足够信息量的代理变量可在不直接观测调整变量的情况下恢复该空间。

Result: 理论上刻画了估计误差与因果误差；建立了完整过程的bootstrap有效性；模拟实验和真实数据应用表明，该方法在性能上优于多种现有替代方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过因果图特异性准则与广义特征值问题的结合，实现了在高维混杂且存在隐变量场景下的稳健因果效应估计，为倾向性评分方法提供了新的理论框架和实践工具。

Abstract: Observational datasets frequently contain many baseline variables, yet investigators estimating causal effects may not know which variables to include in the adjustment set. Confounding information may also be distributed weakly across many variables. Propensity scores can simplify adjustment by reducing high-dimensional covariates to a scalar with binary treatment. Although the propensity score is the coarsest balancing score, this distributional optimality does not imply maximal specificity over causal graphs. We instead examine all causal graphs among a candidate score, treatment, and outcome while allowing latent variables. Under faithfulness, we identify the largest set of unconditional and conditional dependence relations whose truth is invariant to whether treatment causes the outcome, leaving treatment-effect estimation to the downstream analysis. This criterion defines the maximally specific graph class expressible through these relations. We then develop the proposed algorithm, which operationalizes the criterion through a generalized eigenvalue problem whose score space targets the span of a balancing coordinate and an outcome-guided coordinate. We show that sufficiently informative proxies can recover this span without direct observation of the adjustment variables, characterize the resulting estimation and causal errors, and establish bootstrap validity for the complete procedure. Simulations and a real-data application demonstrate superior performance over several alternatives.

</details>


### [347] [Statistical analysis of block structured latent variable models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10328)
*Chengyu Cui,Gongjun Xu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文对分块结构化潜变量模型进行了全面的统计分析，推导了不同分块设计下的模型可识别性条件，通过拉格朗日型公式处理带约束的非凸优化问题，建立了约束最大似然估计量的非渐近误差界和渐近分布，并提出了相应计算框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分块结构化潜变量模型在心理学、教育学、经济学和遗传学等领域广泛应用，但其基本统计性质尚未得到充分研究。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 首先推导不同分块设计下的模型可识别性条件；然后在可识别性约束下研究最大似然估计，引入拉格朗日型公式处理复杂约束的非凸优化问题，证明该公式的最优解与原问题一致；基于此建立约束MLE的锐利非渐近误差界和渐近分布；最后提出估计量的计算框架并建立算法输出的理论性质。

Result: 给出了多种分块配置下的可识别性条件；证明了拉格朗日型公式与原约束优化问题的等价性；建立了约束最大似然估计量的非渐近误差界和渐近分布理论；所提计算框架具有理论保证；通过模拟研究和实证数据分析验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 本文系统建立了分块结构化潜变量模型的统计理论，包括可识别性、估计理论和计算方法，为该类模型在多个领域的应用提供了坚实的理论支撑。

Abstract: This paper studies block structured latent variable models, in which observed variables are grouped into distinct blocks based on their relationships with the underlying latent variables. These block structures are prevalent in various fields such as psychology, education, economics, and genetics. Despite their widespread applications, the fundamental statistical properties of these models remain largely unexplored. In this work, we present a comprehensive statistical analysis of the block structured latent variable models. In particular, we first derive conditions for model identifiability across various block designs. Furthermore, we investigate the maximum likelihood estimation under these identifiability constraints. To accommodate these intricate constraints associated with various block configurations, we introduce a Lagrangian-type formulation for the constrained nonconvex optimization problem and show that its optimum coincides with that of the original problem. This formulation serves as a critical tool for understanding the behavior of the constrained estimator under various block structures. Building on that, we establish sharp non-asymptotic error bounds and asymptotic distributions of the constrained maximum likelihood estimator. We also propose a computational framework to obtain the estimator and establish theoretical properties for the algorithm output. Our theoretical findings are validated through simulation studies and empirical data analyses.

</details>


### [348] [Expert-Guided g-computation with Large Language Models for Estimating Causal Effects on Timings: Applications to Hospital Quality Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10339)
*Patrick Vossler,Jialin Ouyang,F. Richard Guo,Anran Huang,Ali Shojaie,Lucas Zier,Fan Xia,Jean Feng*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出专家引导的g-computation（egg-computation），结合甘特图与因果DAG，用于估计医院质量改进中候选干预措施的平均住院时间节省效果。该方法通过LLM辅助流程实现专家推理的规模化，在模拟和实际医院研究中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医院质量改进项目面临多个候选干预措施的因果效应排序难题。定性方法依赖专家判断，易受认知偏差影响；定量方法依赖数据驱动模型，但在干预措施为假设性（无历史数据）或因果机制复杂需要临床推理时失效。需要一种结合两者优势的方法。

Method: 提出egg-computation框架：首先在甘特图上建立因果模型，将患者轨迹映射为因果DAG；然后使用g-computation变体进行识别，仅在数据不可识别的组件上寻求专家输入；最后开发LLM辅助流程，实现专家推理的可靠规模化。

Result: 模拟研究中，当患者具有多样化因果结构和干预机制时，egg-computation优于传统因果推断方法。在一家城市安全网医院11项候选QI干预措施的研究中，LLM流程生成的图和时间节省估计与人类专家高度一致。

Conclusion: Egg-computation是一个广泛适用的框架，可用于估计任何能用甘特图表示因果机制的候选干预措施的平均时间节省效果，不仅限于医疗领域。

Abstract: Hospital quality improvement (QI) programs routinely face multiple candidate interventions to optimize hospital flow, but existing methods struggle to estimate and rank the causal effects of such interventions. This work focuses on one of the most standard hospital metrics, the average length of stay (LOS), and its causal estimand, the average time saved. To characterize this causal effect, qualitative approaches rely on expert judgment to map patient trajectories, making them susceptible to cognitive biases; quantitative approaches rely on data-driven models, which fail when interventions are hypothetical with no historical data or have complex causal mechanisms that require clinical reasoning rather than data alone. We propose expert-guided g-computation, or egg-computation, which combines the complementary strengths of both approaches by connecting the Gantt charts commonly used to map patient trajectories with the causal DAG literature. We introduce a causal model over Gantt charts and establish identification using a variant of g-computation that seeks expert input only for components unidentifiable from data. To make egg-computation practical, we develop an LLM-assisted pipeline that reliably scales up expert reasoning. In simulations, egg-computation outperforms conventional causal inference methods when patients have diverse causal structures and intervention mechanisms. In a study of eleven candidate QI interventions at an urban safety-net hospital, the LLM pipeline generated graphs and time-saving estimates highly concordant with those of human experts. Beyond healthcare, egg-computation is a broadly applicable framework for estimating the average time saved for candidate interventions whose causal mechanisms can be represented using Gantt charts.

</details>


### [349] [Optimized Sequential Testing for Binary Ensemble Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15237)
*Joseph Kalman,Amit Moscovich*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于序贯检验的集成分类器加速方法：通过线性规划求解最优早停策略，在控制与全集成模型分歧率（如0.1%）的前提下，按序评估基模型并在多数票明确时停止，在多数数据集上取得了4倍以上的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成分类器虽能通过组合更多基模型提升准确率，但训练和推理成本也随之线性增长。现有方法通常需要评估所有基模型后进行多数投票，计算开销大。作者希望在不牺牲分类一致性的前提下，通过序贯评估基模型并提前停止来显著降低计算成本。

Method: 将集成分类的早停问题建模为序贯检验问题，定义三种不同的最优性准则，并在给定可容忍分歧率约束下，通过构造并求解线性规划（Linear Program）高效获得最优停止策略。具体而言，系统按序评估基模型，当累积投票结果足以判定多数类别时立即终止，不再执行剩余基模型。

Result: 在UCI机器学习库的真实数据集以及Grinsztajn等人提出的基准数据集上进行了实验验证。结果表明，在大多数数据集上，所提方法在将分歧率控制在0.1%的条件下可实现4倍或更高的推理加速。

Conclusion: 通过序贯检验与线性规划相结合的方式，可以在几乎不损失分类一致性的前提下大幅降低集成分类器的计算开销，该方法具有理论保证且实践效果显著。

Abstract: Ensemble classifiers are predictive models that combine the results of simpler base models, often by majority vote. A classic example is random forests, which combine the predictions of decision trees. Ensembles that use more base models can be more accurate but also more costly to train and run. In this paper, we consider strategies for reducing the computational cost of binary classification using an approach from the field of sequential testing. Rather than evaluating all the base models and taking a majority vote, we evaluate the base models sequentially and stop execution when a clear majority emerges. We consider three different notions of optimality for early-stopping strategies that minimize the number of base models executed while controlling the rate of disagreement with the full ensemble. For each notion of optimality and allowable disagreement rate, we show that a linear program can be constructed and solved efficiently to find the optimal stopping strategy. We tested these methods on real-world datasets taken from the UC Irvine Machine Learning repository, and on the benchmark datasets proposed by Grinsztajn et al. We found that on most datasets, these methods provide speed-ups of 4x or more while controlling disagreement at 0.1%

</details>


### [350] [Design-Based Prediction-Powered Inference for Spatial Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10356)
*Shinichiro SHirota*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在基于设计的框架下重新构建了预测驱动推断（PPI），推导了简单抽样和分层抽样下的精确设计方差，揭示了空间相关性对覆盖率的影响。核心发现是：当残差存在空间相干依赖时，双稳健性中的剩余项不会随标签数增加而消失；在爱沙尼亚LUCAS数据上，设计匹配的调优比 i.i.d. PPI++ 调优降低了约10%的标准误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PPI理论假设标签是i.i.d.的，但空间标签常来自调查设计或协变量驱动的机制，且地图误差可能存在空间相关性。当残差具有空间相干依赖时，错误指定的倾向得分下，正确结果模型虽能保证超总体识别，但剩余项可能随标签累积而恶化覆盖率。因此需要将PPI重新置于基于设计的框架中，以处理空间抽样设计与空间依赖问题。

Method: 作者将PPI重新构建于基于设计的框架：估计量为固定空间总体的普查参数，随机性来自标签机制。推导了简单随机抽样和分层抽样下的精确设计方差，给出了分块空间平衡是否有益的阈值条件，以及当选择依赖地图时基于估计倾向得分的夹心推断。主要理论结果涉及双稳健性：在错误指定的倾向得分下，正确的结果模型可保证超总体识别，但剩余项量级为 σ_u/√N_{eff,v}，其中 N_{eff,v} 是权重所见的残差斑块有效计数。在比率稳定标签机制下该剩余项与标签数无关。对 i.i.d. 或可交换残差场，N_{eff,v} 量级为 N；空间相干依赖则使剩余项与抽样误差绑定。

Result: 在48,175个格点的全枚举总体上的模拟验证了理论：空间相干依赖下剩余项确实与抽样误差绑定。爱沙尼亚LUCAS数据的七个土地覆盖估计量应用表明：i.i.d. PPI++调优对最佳地图反而降低精度，而设计匹配的调优将标准误降低约10%，并在所有七个估计量上匹配或优于PPI++。汇总残差诊断可能将空间结构化的层间变异误判为残差依赖。

Conclusion: 空间应用中的PPI必须在基于设计的框架下进行，功效调优和依赖诊断都必须尊重抽样设计。空间相干残差依赖会破坏标准双稳健性中剩余项可忽略的结论，需采用设计匹配的调优策略来保证推断有效性。

Abstract: Prediction-powered inference (PPI) combines a wall-to-wall prediction map with a small gold-standard sample to give confidence intervals valid whatever the map's quality. Canonical PPI theory starts from i.i.d.\ labelling, whereas spatial labels arrive through survey designs or covariate-driven mechanisms, and map errors may be spatially correlated. We recast PPI in a design-based framework: the estimand is a census parameter of a fixed spatial population, with randomness arising from the labelling mechanism. We derive exact design variances under simple and stratified sampling, a threshold for when blocked spatial balance pays, and sandwich inference for estimated propensities when selection depends on the map. Our main result concerns double robustness. With a misspecified propensity, a correct outcome model secures superpopulation identification but, conditional on the realised population, leaves a remainder of order $σ_u/\sqrt{N_{\mathrm{eff},v}}$, an effective count of the residual patches the weights see. Under ratio-stable labelling this remainder is free of the label count, so coverage can deteriorate as labels accumulate. For i.i.d.\ or exchangeable residual fields $N_{\mathrm{eff},v}$ is of order $N$ and the remainder is negligible beside sampling error when $n/N \to 0$; spatially coherent dependence instead makes it bind. We reproduce this on a fully enumerated population of $48{,}175$ cells. Estonian LUCAS applications show that power tuning and dependence diagnostics must respect the design: i.i.d.\ PPI++ tuning worsens precision for the best map, whereas design-matched tuning cuts standard errors by about $10\%$ and matches or beats PPI++ across seven land-cover estimands. Pooled residual diagnostics can likewise mistake spatially structured between-stratum variation for residual dependence.

</details>


### [351] [Duality and Error for Predictively Oriented Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10592)
*Aurya Javeed,Drew P. Kouri,Teresa Portone,Rebekah White*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究预测导向（PrO）推断，通过对数评分规则与φ-散度正则化，推导出有限维对偶形式，建立了零对偶间隙准则，并给出有限样本超额预测风险界，辅以可解示例验证理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯推断在模型误设下可能失效，PrO推断通过直接优化预测性能来应对模型误设，但其理论性质（如对偶结构、风险界）尚不完善，需要系统分析。

Method: 采用对数评分规则与一般φ-散度正则化框架：1）推导PrO推断的有限维对偶问题（n+1个变量）；2）建立零对偶间隙准则与最优性条件，得到PrO后验的半解析表示；3）推导有限样本超额预测风险界；4）用可解分类示例和高斯位置混合示例验证。

Result: 对偶问题仅含n+1变量；KL正则化下后验具有指数形式；得到将抽样波动、散度预算下近似、正则化与数值优化误差分离的有限样本风险界；分类示例表明预测风险收敛可推出唯一预测分布，即使参数分布无弱极限；不同φ-散度需不同正则化调度。

Conclusion: PrO推断的有限维对偶结构为数值求解提供了便利，KL正则化产生指数族后验，风险界为近似推断提供了理论保障，模型误设下预测分布收敛性不依赖于参数空间的弱收敛。

Abstract: Predictively oriented (PrO) inference quantifies uncertainty by selecting a distribution over model parameters to optimize a scoring rule applied to the induced predictive distribution, together with a divergence penalty from a reference distribution. By applying the scoring rule after averaging model densities, PrO inference targets predictive performance, accounting for model misspecification. We focus on the logarithmic score with general $φ$-divergence regularization. Our contributions are twofold. First, we derive a finite-dimensional dual formulation of PrO inference. For $n$ observations, the dual problem has $n+1$ variables. We establish zero-duality-gap criteria and optimality conditions that relate the primal and dual solutions. When primal and dual solutions exist, these conditions yield a semi-analytical representation of the PrO posterior and certificates for assessing the accuracy of numerical solutions. For Kullback--Leibler regularization, the posterior has an exponential form. Second, we derive a finite-sample excess predictive-risk bound for approximate PrO posteriors that separates sampling fluctuation, approximation under a divergence budget, regularization, and numerical optimization error. The result applies even when the benchmark predictive risk is not attained by any probability distribution over the model parameters having finite divergence from the reference distribution. We use an exactly solvable categorical example to show that predictive-risk convergence can imply convergence to a unique predictive distribution even though the parameter distributions have no weak limit on the original parameter space. The example also shows that different $φ$-divergences can require different regularization schedules. We conclude with a misspecified Gaussian location-mixture example that illustrates the dual computation, primal recovery, and numerical accuracy checks.

</details>


### [352] [Survey-robust uncertainty quantification in generalised additive models for location, scale, and shape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10727)
*Dennis Meurer,Timo Adam*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对复杂调查数据中的GAMLSS模型，提出了一种基于线性化的夹层方差估计量，能够纳入调查设计信息，在保持计算效率的同时提供准确的标准误估计和可靠的置信区间覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的仅均值回归模型无法满足复杂调查数据中研究者对响应分布其他方面（如尺度、形状参数）的兴趣。GAMLSS虽然允许所有分布参数依赖协变量，但现有标准误估计方法均未考虑复杂调查设计，导致参数估计的变异性被严重低估。

Method: 提出一种基于线性化的夹层方差估计量（linearisation-based sandwich variance estimator），该方法将调查设计（如分层、聚类、加权）纳入估计过程，同时保持计算上的高效性，作为重复抽样方法（replication-based methods）的替代方案。

Result: 基于合成调查数据的模拟研究表明，所提估计量对所有分布参数均能提供准确的标准误估计和可靠的置信区间覆盖，且相比重复抽样方法具有更高的计算效率。通过2019/20年卢旺达人口与健康调查（DHS）数据的再分析进一步验证了方法的实用性。

Conclusion: 该线性化夹层方差估计量为复杂调查数据中的GAMLSS建模提供了一种既考虑设计效应又计算高效的推断工具，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Conventional mean-only regression models are often too restrictive for the analysis of complex survey data, where interest frequently extends beyond the conditional mean to other aspects of the response distribution. Generalised additive models for location, scale and shape (GAMLSS) provide a flexible framework by allowing all distributional parameters to depend on covariates. However, existing model-based and model-robust standard errors fail to account for complex survey designs and can substantially underestimate the variability of regression parameter estimates. We propose a linearisation-based sandwich variance estimator that incorporates the survey design while remaining computationally efficient. Using a simulation study based on synthetic survey data, we demonstrate that the proposed estimator provides accurate standard error estimates and reliable confidence interval coverage for all distributional parameters, while offering a computationally efficient alternative to replication-based methods. We further illustrate the practical utility of the approach through a re-analysis of data from the 2019/20 Rwanda Demographic and Health Survey (DHS).

</details>


### [353] [Win-Ratio Regression for Prioritized Composite Outcomes in Observational Studies: Doubly Robust and Efficient Estimation with Future-Score Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10728)
*Zhuochao Huang,Lucy Shao,Yi Guo,Xin M. Tu,Changyong Feng,Tuo Lin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对分层优先化配对结局的赢率回归框架，核心创新是"未来得分校正"方法——当删失阻止后续配对比较时，用给定已观测历史的条件期望替代剩余得分。结合治疗加权和基线结局增广，实现了治疗分配和删失的双重稳健性。模拟显示在高删失率下效率增益显著，65%删失时相对效率达1.50。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在临床研究中，事件常遵循自然层级（如死亡优先于住院），此时优先化配对结局非常有用。然而，配对未解决前的删失（censoring）使得估计复杂化。现有方法的不足在于：逆删失加权（IPCW）仅利用了部分信息，无法充分恢复被删失掩盖的配对比较信息，尤其在高删失率下效率损失严重。

Method: 作者提出AIPW-FC（Augmented Inverse Probability Weighting with Future-score Correction）估计框架：(1) 定义完整数据的赢率回归目标函数；(2) 为观测数据导出估计方程；(3) 核心步骤——未来得分校正（FC）：当删失阻止后续配对比较时，用给定已观测历史的剩余得分的条件期望进行填补，恢复IPCW无法捕获的配对信息；(4) 引入治疗加权和基线结局增广以控制基线混杂；(5) 基于U统计量理论推导推断性质，证明所有讨厌函数正确指定时估计量渐近正态且有效。

Result: 理论层面，AIPW-FC估计量满足渐近正态性，且在讨厌函数均正确指定时达到有效。模拟研究：在30%、50%、65%三种删失率下，未来得分校对的效率增益随删失率增加而上升；65%删失下相对效率达1.50；AIPW-FC的覆盖率接近名义水平。OneFlorida电子健康记录数据应用验证了方法在处理以死亡优先于住院的复合结局时的实用性。

Conclusion: 未来得分校正为处理优先化配对结局中的删失问题提供了一种信息利用更充分的方法，相比传统IPCW具有显著的效率提升。该方法结合增广逆概率加权后实现了双重稳健性，在高删失率的实际临床数据中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Prioritized pairwise outcomes are useful when clinical events follow a natural hierarchy, but censoring before pair resolution complicates estimation. We develop a win-ratio regression framework for this setting by defining a complete-data target over follow-up and deriving an estimating equation for the observed data. The central idea is future-score correction (FC): when censoring prevents later pairwise comparisons from being observed, the method replaces the remaining score with its conditional expectation given the observed history. This correction recovers pairwise information beyond that provided by inverse censoring weights alone. Additionally, we incorporate treatment weighting and baseline outcome augmentation to address baseline confounding. Together, these components yield double robustness for treatment assignment and censoring. Inference is obtained from U-statistic theory. Under standard regularity conditions, the AIPW-FC estimator is asymptotically normal and efficient when all nuisance functions are correctly specified. Simulations with 30%, 50%, and 65% censoring show that efficiency gains from future-score correction increase with the censoring rate, with relative efficiency reaching 1.50 under 65% censoring and near-nominal coverage for AIPW-FC. An application to OneFlorida electronic health record data illustrates the method for a composite outcome that prioritizes death over hospitalization.

</details>


### [354] [When Is a General Factor Distinguishable? Non-Proportionality, Stable Structure, and the Bifactor Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10731)
*Jinsong Chen*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文探讨了双因子模型与相关一阶因子模型之间在总体协方差矩阵层面的可区分性问题，证明可区分性是一个分级属性，取决于载荷比例是否成立，并提出了一种在部分探索性因子分析中的两步程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子分析中，究竟需要引入一个额外的"一般因子"（双因子模型），还是相关的一阶因子已足够，这一问题在实证中常被混淆。现有方法多依赖拟合指标或估计器特性，但作者指出这本质上是总体协方差矩阵的属性问题，需要从理论上厘清在什么条件下二者可区分。

Method: 作者通过理论推导（命题1与定理1）刻画了双因子结构与相关因子模型的协方差等价条件：当每个聚类内一般载荷与组载荷成比例时，二者协方差等价、无法区分；当成比例关系在每个聚类中都不成立时，三个条目加适度正则条件即可使双因子结构无法被任何K因子对角唯一性模型复现。中间存在混合边界，由"聚类抵抗"决定。基于此，提出一种两步程序：在部分探索性因子分析框架下，仅在结构跨相邻因子数复现时才输出结构，否则视为"无法判定"。

Result: 模拟研究表明，一致的因子数判定可能伴随无法复现的结构；被吸收的局部依赖性可伪装成一般因子，且该错误随样本量增大而加剧，而稳定性指标却保持"干净"。四个实证数据集展示了该程序可能出现的不同结果。

Conclusion: 可区分性是分级的、依赖于总体到K因子类的距离。提出以"跨相邻计数的可复现性"作为结构交付的条件，将"不交付"视为合理结果，有助于避免将局部依赖误判为一般因子。

Abstract: Whether an additional general dimension is necessary beyond correlated first-order factors is a property of the population covariance matrix, not of any estimator or design. This research establishes when that property can be decided. Where the general and group loadings are proportional within every cluster the bifactor structure is covariance-equivalent to correlated factors, so no sample size separates them (Proposition 1); where that proportionality fails in every cluster, three items per cluster and some mild regularities leave no $K$-factor model with diagonal uniquenesses able to reproduce the covariance matrix (Theorem 1); and between them lies a mixed boundary, located numerically here and turning on cluster resistance. Distinguishability is therefore graded, measured by the population distance to the $K$-factor class. Because that question is conditional on a first-order structure which is itself uncertain, a two-step procedure is developed within partially exploratory factor analysis, delivering a structure only when it reproduces across adjacent counts and treating non-delivery as legitimate. Simulation shows that a unanimous count can accompany a structure that fails to reproduce, and that absorbed local dependence can imitate a general factor, the error growing with sample size while stability indicators stay clean. Four empirical datasets illustrate the possible outcomes.

</details>


### [355] [Optimal Stratification of a Sampling Frame: A Comparative Study of Classical, Quantum, and Quantum-Inspired Approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10787)
*Marco Ballin,Giulio Barcaroli*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文比较了四种求解器（D-Wave模拟退火、IBM QAOA、QCi Dirac-3熵量子计算、经典GPU Ising机）在最优分层问题上的表现，以20层瑞士市镇数据为案例。结果显示经典遗传算法（129样本单元）仍为最优，最佳QUBO解对应160样本量，与基准存在约24%差距；IBM和Dirac-3的表现更差，分别对应262和286样本量。差距源于离散度代理目标与真实Bethel-Chromy目标的偏差，以及各求解器的平台限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优分层的组合优化目标可重新表述为二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题。本研究旨在通过20层真实数据的案例，比较不同量子及经典求解器在异构免费层级约束下求解该QUBO代理问题的实际表现，评估量子方法相对于经典方法在样本量最小化任务上的可行性与差距。

Method: 以瑞士市镇数据集（swissmunicipalities）20层抽样框为统一测试平台，比较四种求解器：(1) D-Wave公式化QUBO（因QPU不可用，采用模拟退火求解）；(2) IBM Quantum门基量子处理器运行QAOA算法；(3) QCi Dirac-3光子熵量子计算设备；(4) Fixstars Amplify AE经典GPU基Ising机作为对照。以遗传算法（129样本单元）为基准，并采用Bethel-Chromy评估将QUBO目标值映射为实际样本量。各求解器在硬件、计算预算、迭代次数及编码方式上均不同，故为案例研究而非受控实验。

Result: 遗传算法基准为129样本单元；已知最佳QUBO解（目标值501.0）经Bethel-Chromy评估后对应160样本量，与基准差距约24%，且该解为已发现最优解而非认证最优。IBM QAOA结果对应262样本量，Dirac-3对应286样本量，均显著劣于经典方法。IBM结果与稀疏量子比特格上的SWAP路由退化一致（但无法排除其他原因）；Dirac-3虽实现了真正的优化，但未能达到免费GPU数秒内即可获得的解的质量。

Conclusion: 在测试规模下，经典遗传算法仍为最优分层的方法首选。原因有二：(a) 离散度代理目标与真实Bethel-Chromy目标之间存在固有差距，后者无法编码为低次多项式；(b) IBM和Dirac-3运行中存在求解器及平台特定限制。上述发现对任一结果均未确立全局最优性。

Abstract: Optimal stratification aggregates strata into a small number of final strata to minimise total sample size required to meet target precision constraints. This combinatorial objective, reformulated as a within-cluster dispersion surrogate, can be expressed as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimisation (QUBO) problem. This paper reports a comparative case study of four solvers for that surrogate, run under heterogeneous free-tier constraints on an identical twenty-stratum frame from the swissmunicipalities dataset: a D-Wave-formulated QUBO, solved here by simulated annealing (QPU access unavailable); a gate-based quantum processor (IBM Quantum, running QAOA); a photonic entropy-quantum-computing device (QCi Dirac-3); and a classical GPU-based Ising machine as control (Fixstars Amplify AE). Solvers differ in hardware, computational budget, iteration count, and, for IBM, the encoding itself, so the comparison is a case study rather than a controlled experiment. Against a genetic-algorithm benchmark of 129 sample units, the best-known QUBO solution (objective 501.0) maps, after Bethel-Chromy evaluation, to a sample size of 160, a gap of roughly 24%, tied to the best solution found rather than a certified optimum. IBM and Dirac-3 fall further short, at 262 and 286; the IBM result is consistent with SWAP-routing degradation on a sparse qubit lattice, though not isolated from other causes, while Dirac-3, despite genuine optimisation, settles short of what a free GPU reaches in seconds. The paper concludes that a classical genetic algorithm remains, at the scale tested, the method of choice for optimal stratification, reflecting (a) limits of the dispersion surrogate versus the true Bethel-Chromy objective, not encodable as a low-degree polynomial, and (b) solver- and platform-specific limits in the IBM and Dirac-3 runs. These findings do not establish global optimality for either result.

</details>


### [356] [Goodness-of-Fit Tests and Calibration Machine-Learning Algorithms for Logistic Regression with Sparse Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11140)
*Ebrahim Khaled Ebrahim*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本论文系统比较了约30种二分类逻辑回归的拟合优度检验方法和机器学习校准算法，涵盖经典卡方检验、Hosmer-Lemeshow变体、标准化Pearson统计量、协变量空间划分法、平滑方法及现代校准机器学习与自助法。在固定样本量下，GiViTI校准检验、McCullagh、Osius-Rojek、le Cessie和Stute-Zhu等方法在统计功效和I类错误控制方面表现优异。论文强调，正式检验须与可视化诊断（如校准图）结合使用，才能全面评估模型拟合质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑回归模型的拟合优度检验是推断前的关键步骤，但在连续型预测变量（如年龄、体重）导致数据"稀疏"时，传统方法（如卡方检验和偏差检验）因渐近分布假设不满足而失效。这一普遍问题亟需系统性的方法比较与评估。

Method: 在分组数据和稀疏数据两种场景下，对约30种统计检验和机器学习校准算法进行了系统性实证比较，包括：经典卡方及Hosmer-Lemeshow变体、标准化Pearson统计量、协变量空间划分法、基于平滑的方法，以及现代校准机器学习与自助法程序。使用Low Birth Weight真实数据集进行应用验证。

Result: 在固定样本量下，GiViTI校准检验（2016）、McCullagh（1989）、Osius-Rojek（1992）、le Cessie（1995）和Stute-Zhu（2002）五种方法在正确识别不良模型（高经验功效）与不对良好模型误报警（正确的经验I类错误）之间取得了最佳平衡。真实数据应用表明，许多检验在面对实际数据复杂性时无法给出有效结论；可视化诊断（如校准图）能发现正式检验常忽略的模型缺陷。

Conclusion: 模型评估不能依赖单一方法，必须将多种高功效统计检验与模型校准的仔细可视化检查相结合，才能对逻辑回归模型的拟合质量做出可靠判断。

Abstract: Assessing the goodness-of-fit of a logistic regression model is a critical prerequisite before the model is used for inference. However, goodness-of-fit (GOF) tests such as the chi-square and deviance tests often give invalid results when the data are "sparse" -- a common issue with continuous predictors like age or weight, where the asymptotic distributional assumptions are not satisfied. This thesis studies classical GOF tests for binary logistic regression under both grouped and sparse data, comparing about 30 statistical tests and machine-learning calibration algorithms. These span the classical chi-square and Hosmer-Lemeshow variants, standardized Pearson statistics, covariate-space partitioning, smoothing-based methods, and contemporary calibration machine-learning and bootstrap procedures. At a fixed size, the GiViTI calibration test (2016), McCullagh (1989), Osius-Rojek (1992), le Cessie (1995) and Stute-Zhu (2002) proved empirically powerful, balancing correct identification of bad models (high empirical power) against not raising false alarms on good models (correct empirical Type I error). Relying on formal methods alone is insufficient: visual diagnostics such as calibration plots are a vital exploratory step for detecting model deficiencies that formal tests often overlook. An application to real data (the Low Birth Weight dataset) shows that many of these tests fail to give valid conclusions when exposed to the complexities of actual datasets. The main conclusion is that model assessment requires a combination of several powerful statistical tests alongside careful visual inspection of model calibration.

</details>


### [357] [Endogeneity-Aware Cognitive Diagnostic Model for Multidomain Ordinal Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10913)
*Zhiyu Huang,Jing Ouyang,Kai Kang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种内生性感知的认知诊断模型（EACDM），用于多变量有序评估。该模型结合了分块结构的诊断测量成分和逻辑结构成分，能够在不破坏领域特定测量结构的前提下，研究诊断属性之间的内生关系。作者建立了模型的可识别性条件，开发了MCMC估计算法，并通过模拟研究和帕金森病实际数据验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的认知诊断模型（CDMs）虽然能为潜在属性轮廓提供可解释的测量模型，但它们通常无法刻画来自不同领域的潜在属性之间的有向依赖关系（即内生性）。当这种内生性存在时，传统CDMs可能无法恢复真实结构，因此需要一个新的建模框架来同时处理测量结构与属性间的内生关系。

Method: 提出内生性感知的认知诊断模型（EACDM），其测量部分采用分块对角Q矩阵将题组与领域特定的二值属性关联，结构部分则采用logistic回归将一组属性对另一组属性及协变量进行回归，同时考虑潜在分类的不确定性。作者建立了Q矩阵、有效载荷、潜在轮廓概率及结构系数的可识别性条件，并开发了马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（MCMC）算法对测量和结构成分进行联合估计。

Result: 模拟研究表明，EACDM能够准确恢复项目参数、潜在结构和结构系数；而传统CDMs在内生性存在时无法恢复真实情况。在帕金森病数据的应用中，该方法有效揭示了非运动潜在特质与运动损伤轮廓之间的内在关联。

Conclusion: EACDM为多领域有序评估中诊断属性之间的内生关系研究提供了一个简洁且有效的框架，兼具测量解释性与结构建模能力，在存在内生性的场景下显著优于传统CDMs。

Abstract: Multidomain assessment batteries generate ordinal item responses that are often summarized through latent attribute profiles. Conventional cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) provide interpretable measurement models for such profiles, but they typically do not represent directed dependence among latent attributes from distinct domains. We propose an endogeneity-aware cognitive diagnostic model (EACDM) for multivariate ordinal assessments. The model combines a block-structured diagnostic measurement component, in which item groups are linked to domain-specific binary attributes through a block-diagonal Q-matrix, with a logistic structural component, in which one attribute block is regressed on another block and subject-level covariates while accounting for latent classification uncertainty. This formulation yields a parsimonious framework for studying endogenous relationships among diagnostic attributes without collapsing domain-specific measurement structure. We establish identifiability conditions for the Q-matrix, effective loadings, latent-profile probabilities, and structural coefficients, and develop a Markov chain Monte Carlo algorithm for joint estimation of the measurement and structural components. Simulation studies demonstrate accurate recovery of item parameters, latent structures, and structural coefficients for the proposed EACDM, whereas conventional CDMs can fail to recover the ground truth when endogeneity is present. We apply the proposed method to Parkinson's disease data to examine how non-motor latent traits relate to motor impairment profiles.

</details>


### [358] [Finite-sample correction for the covariate-adjusted log-rank test](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10923)
*Pavla Krotka,Dominic Magirr*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对协变量校正对数秩检验在小样本下的一类错误膨胀问题，提出了一种有限样本校正方法，通过对检验统计量分母的修正，考虑残差自由度的损失及回归系数估计的不确定性，显著降低了多种场景下的一类错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量校正对数秩检验虽能保证相对于标准对数秩检验的效率增益，但在小样本情况下会出现一类错误率膨胀，尤其在分配不平衡或协变量数量相对样本量较大时更为严重。现有方法缺乏针对该问题的有限样本校正手段。

Method: 提出对协变量校正对数秩检验统计量的分母进行有限样本校正，具体修正方案纳入两方面因素：(1) 残差自由度的损失；(2) 未知回归系数估计的不确定性。通过调整分母使得检验统计量在小样本下更准确地近似其理论分布。

Result: 模拟研究表明，应用该校正方法后，在多种场景下（包括不同样本量、分配比例及协变量数量组合）一类错误率膨胀均得到显著降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的有限样本校正是解决协变量校正对数秩检验小样本一类错误膨胀问题的有效方法，拓宽了该方法在随机对照试验中的适用性，尤其对不平衡分配和高维协变量设置的试验具有重要实用价值。

Abstract: The covariate-adjusted log-rank test is a novel method for covariate adjustment in randomized trials with time-to-event endpoints, offering guaranteed efficiency gains compared to the standard log-rank test. However, it has been noted that, in small samples, this method may lead to type I error rate inflation. This issue is particularly pronounced in trials with imbalanced allocation and settings where the number of adjustment covariates is large relative to the sample size. We propose a finite-sample correction for the denominator of the covariate-adjusted log-rank test statistic that accounts for the loss of the residual degrees of freedom as well as the uncertainty in the unknown regression coefficients. In simulations, we show that applying this correction leads to a substantial reduction in the type I error rate inflation across multiple scenarios.

</details>


### [359] [A parametric framework for assessing and estimating sufficient follow-up time in cure models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11029)
*Luiz Silva-Resende,Dionisio Alves-Neto,Danilo Alvares,Vera Tomazella*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的参数框架，将Parametric Follow-up Sufficiency Test与两种基于容差的准则（Plateau Distance Criterion和Residual Survival Criterion）相结合，既能评估现有随访的充分性，又能量化所需的最短额外随访时间，并在Weibull混合治愈模型下给出了解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献存在两个割裂的研究方向：一是评估随访充分性的推断性方法，二是基于超額危险、条件治愈概率或残差生存定义估计治愈时间的方法。前者无法量化在明确容差下所需的额外随访时间，后者则缺乏对现有随访的正式推断评估。作者旨在弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: 作者在Weibull混合治愈模型框架下，结合Parametric Follow-up Sufficiency Test（比较末端Kaplan-Meier估计与参数混合治愈模型估计的治愈率），提出两种互补的容差准则：Plateau Distance Criterion（总体生存尺度）和Residual Survival Criterion（易感者生存尺度），并推导出两种准则的闭合形式表达式，证明在容差参数适当变换下两者给出相同的最短随访时间。

Result: 通过Monte Carlo模拟评估了框架在不同样本量、治愈率和行政删失场景下的有限样本表现。在前列腺癌和三阴性乳腺癌数据上的应用表明，该框架能有效评估现有随访充分性、估计给定容差下所需的额外观察时间，并识别随访已充分的场景。

Conclusion: 该统一框架填补了随访充分性评估与治愈时间估计之间的方法学空白，为临床研究者提供了兼具推断功能与可操作容差指导的实用工具。

Abstract: Reliable estimation of cure fractions depends critically on adequate follow-up. Classical procedures for assessing follow-up sufficiency are primarily inferential, whereas a separate body of literature estimates time to cure using excess-hazard, conditional cure-probability, or residual-survival definitions. These two approaches remain largely disconnected: assessing the adequacy of observed follow-up does not generally quantify the additional follow-up required under an explicit tolerance, whereas estimating time to cure does not itself provide a formal inferential assessment of the available follow-up. We develop a unified parametric framework that combines the Parametric Follow-up Sufficiency Test, which compares the terminal Kaplan-Meier estimate with the cure fraction estimated from a parametric mixture cure model, with two complementary tolerance-based formulations: the Plateau Distance Criterion on the population survival scale and the Residual Survival Criterion on the susceptible survival scale. We derive closed-form expressions for both criteria under the Weibull mixture cure model and establish that they yield identical minimum follow-up times under an appropriate transformation of their tolerance parameters. We evaluate the finite-sample performance of the framework through Monte Carlo simulations across sample sizes, cure fractions, and administrative censoring scenarios. Applications to prostate cancer and triple-negative breast cancer data illustrate how the framework assesses available follow-up, estimates additional observation time under prespecified tolerances, and identifies settings in which follow-up is already adequate.

</details>


### [360] [Resampling simplicial depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11131)
*Carsten Jentsch,Stanislav Nagy,Martin Wendler*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究二维空间中样本单纯形深度(SD)的渐近行为，证明其U统计量在x为P的对称中心时以速率n退化，否则以√n非退化。针对对称中心未知导致分布推断困难的问题，提出一种基于子采样的两阶段自适应程序：先通过偏差校正估计收敛速率参数γ∈{1/2,1}，再利用该估计逼近样本SD的分布，并证明该方法的相合性及其在置信区间构建和监督分类中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单纯形深度(SD)的渐近分布具有双重模态：当x为分布P的对称中心时，样本SD作为U统计量是退化的（收敛速率为n）；否则为非退化（收敛速率为√n）。由于对称中心通常未知，研究者无法预先判断应采用哪种渐近分布，这给样本SD的分布估计和统计推断带来严重困难。需要一种能自适应识别收敛速率并正确估计分布的方法。

Method: 首先在二维情形(d=2)下建立理论条件，证明SD的U统计量退化当且仅当x为P的对称中心。然后提出基于子采样(subsampling)的两阶段适应过程：(1)第一阶段，利用子采样构建速率参数γ∈{1/2,1}的估计量γ̂，采用针对U统计量的偏差校正技术，使n^γ正确刻画SD的收敛速率；(2)第二阶段，利用γ̂逼近样本SD的分布。整个过程无需预先知道对称中心位置即可自适应完成分布估计。

Result: 理论上在温和条件下严格证明了二维SD的退化与非退化二分行为。方法上提出的子采样程序具有相合性(consistency)。应用层面展示了该方法在两个场景中的有效性：构建SD的置信区间，以及基于SD的监督分类任务。

Conclusion: 论文解决了样本单纯形深度因对称中心未知而导致的分布推断难题。通过理论证明明确了二维情形下SD退化的充要条件，并提出了自适应子采样方法，能够一致地估计SD的分布，在置信区间构建和分类任务中具有实用价值。

Abstract: The simplicial depth (SD) is a commonly used indicator of the centrality of points $x\in\mathbb{R}^d$ with respect to distributions $P$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$. Asymptotic theory for the sample SD is based on its representation as a $U$-statistic, which can be either non-degenerate or degenerate. For $d=2$, we prove under mild conditions that this $U$-statistic is degenerate with rate $n$ if and only if $x$ is a center of symmetry of $P$. Otherwise, the asymptotic distribution of SD is non-degenerate with rate $\sqrt{n}$. Because the location of the center of symmetry of $P$ is usually unknown, these two modes of behavior complicate the estimation of the sample distribution of SD at $x$.
  We propose a two-step adaptive subsampling procedure for estimating that distribution. First, an estimator $\widehat γ$ of a parameter $γ\in\{1/2, 1\}$ characterizing the correct rate of convergence $n^γ$ of SD is constructed based on subsampling. Our estimator uses a bias correction suitable for $U$-statistics. Second, $\widehat γ$ is employed for approximating the distribution of the sample SD. We prove the consistency of this subsampling approach and illustrate its usefulness (i) in the construction of confidence intervals for SD, and (ii) in an SD-based supervised classification task

</details>


### [361] [Detection coherence of tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11177)
*Marian Grendar*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个评估统计检验“检测相干性”的框架，揭示了许多经典非参数检验（如Wilcoxon–Mann–Whitney、Kruskal–Wallis、Friedman、logrank）存在“盲点”——即检验统计量所能检测的备择假设范围（检测-零集）超出了该检验名义上校准的零假设范围，导致这些检验在拒绝和不拒绝时均可能产生误导性推断。作者主张应弃用缺乏检测相干性的检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多常用统计检验的零假设与其检验统计量实际可检测的偏离范围（检测-零集）之间存在错配，即检验实际“能看到”的备择假设超出了它名义上声称要检验的零假设。这种错配导致检验的盲点：在该盲点内，无论样本量多大，检验都会系统性地漏检真实效应，或在拒绝时产生虚假发现。过去缺乏一个系统的框架来诊断和评估这一问题。

Method: 提出了一套全新的概念框架，包括校准统计量（calibration statistic）、检测器（detector）、检测泛函（detection functional）、检测-零集（detection-null set）和盲点（blind spot）。基于这些概念，定义了检测相干性（detection coherence）：即盲点是否为空。利用该框架系统分析多类经典非参数检验：Wilcoxon–Mann–Whitney、Kruskal–Wallis、Friedman、logrank检验被证明在无限制条件下是检测不相干的；而Kolmogorov–Smirnov、Zaremba检验以及基于特征核的最大均值差异（MMD）检验被证明是普遍检测相干的。还讨论了通过领域限制消除盲点的可能性和局限性。

Result: Wilcoxon–Mann–Whitney、Kruskal–Wallis、Friedman和logrank检验作为无限制检验时是检测不相干的，存在非空盲点。因盲点的存在，这些检验在不拒绝时会漏掉发现，在拒绝时可能产生虚假发现，且这两个问题均与样本量无关。Kolmogorov–Smirnov、Zaremba和带特征核的MMD检验是普遍检测相干的。通过领域限制虽可使不相干检验变得相干，但该方法在实践中并不可靠。

Conclusion: 检测不相干的检验因其内在的盲点问题，无论在拒绝还是不拒绝的情况下都可能产生误导性结论，且样本量增大无法消除该问题。作者明确主张应弃用此类检验，转而使用普遍检测相干的替代方法（如Kolmogorov–Smirnov、MMD等），以确保统计推断的逻辑一致性。

Abstract: There exist tests calibrated under a null narrower than the one implied by their test statistic -- the detection-null set. The part of the detection-null set not in the null is the test's blind spot. A framework for assessing detection coherence -- whether a test's blind spot is empty -- is introduced, built on new concepts of calibration statistic, detector, detection functional, detection-null set, and blind spot. It is demonstrated that the Wilcoxon--Mann--Whitney, Kruskal--Wallis, Friedman, and logrank tests are detection incoherent as unrestricted tests. Detection incoherent tests may become coherent under restrictions that make the blind spot empty, though domain restriction is not a reliable route to coherence in practice. The Kolmogorov--Smirnov and Zaremba tests, and the Maximum Mean Discrepancy test with a characteristic kernel, are shown to be universally detection coherent. Due to the blind spot, a detection incoherent test misses discoveries when non-rejecting and yields spurious discoveries when rejecting, in both cases regardless of sample size. Detection incoherent tests should be abandoned.

</details>


### [362] [Bayesian inference on beta diversity via feature allocation models with imperfect detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11180)
*Federica Stolf,Tommaso Rigon,David B. Dunson*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的贝叶斯特征分配模型，用于分析具有不完全探测的、部分可交换的物种出现数据。该框架结合潜特征分配与基于占用的探测机制，能够显式处理假阴性并发现新物种，推导出物种共享和beta多样性的后验及预测分布，在高维生物多样性研究中实现可扩展推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Beta多样性的统计推断面临三大挑战：物种出现数据高维、大量物种未被观测、调查存在不完全探测。现有方法要么依赖经验相异度指数且不确定性量化不足，要么基于不切实际的可交换性和完美探测假设。亟需一个能同时解决这些问题的统一概率框架。

Method: 提出一类新的贝叶斯特征分配模型，核心是将潜特征分配模型与基于占用的探测机制相结合。具体而言：(1) 利用潜特征分配模型刻画跨样点的异质性物种组成；(2) 嵌入占用模型以显式建模不完全探测过程；(3) 推导物种共享数量及未来抽样期望共享物种数的显式后验和预测分布；(4) 通过解析结果实现高维场景下的可扩展推断。

Result: 仿真研究表明，该方法在物种共享和群落间多样性推断方面优于现有方法。在全球真菌生物多样性数据的实际应用中，进一步验证了该方法对物种共享和群落间异质性推断的改进效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为不完全探测下的beta多样性推断提供了一致、可解释的概率解决方案，能够产生具有不确定性量化的后验摘要，适用于高维生物多样性研究，并为未来抽样提供预测分布。

Abstract: Beta diversity quantifies variation in species composition across ecological communities and is fundamental for understanding biodiversity patterns across space and environmental gradients. Statistical inference on beta diversity is challenging: species occurrence data are high dimensional, many species remain unobserved despite extensive sampling, and surveys are subject to imperfect detection. Existing approaches are typically based on empirical dissimilarity indices with limited uncertainty quantification or on models that rely on unrealistic exchangeability and perfect detection assumptions. We introduce a new class of Bayesian feature allocation models for partially exchangeable species occurrence data with imperfect detection. The framework combines latent feature allocation models with occupancy-based detection mechanisms, allowing heterogeneous species compositions across sites, explicitly accounting for false negatives, and accommodating the discovery of previously unobserved species. We develop coherent probabilistic inference for species sharing and beta diversity, deriving explicit posterior and predictive distributions for between-community heterogeneity, including the number of shared species across sites and the number expected under future sampling. These analytical results yield interpretable posterior summaries of compositional heterogeneity and facilitate scalable inference in high-dimensional biodiversity studies. Simulation studies and an application to global fungal biodiversity data demonstrate improved inference on species sharing and between-community diversity.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [363] [Deciding When to Switch: E-Processes for Adaptive Minimax Training for Generative Adversarial Nets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10096)
*Hyunjoo Kim,Sicheng Wu,Agastya Venkatraman,Guang Lin,Sehwan Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将GAN训练中判别器与生成器之间的切换问题形式化为序贯假设检验，提出基于e-process的自适应训练框架。在判别器更新阶段，检验生成分布与真实分布分离度是否低于目标水平；在生成器更新阶段，检验该分离度是否仍高于刷新水平。该方法提供随时有效的I类错误控制，并在多个基准上匹配或超越最优固定比率基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据科学中越来越多的问题无法自然纳入预设统计模型的参数框架，典型如优化算法的动态评估——训练中需决策何时切换更新阶段。GAN训练尤为突出：现有方法依赖固定更新比率或启发式准则，缺乏原则性的切换决策机制。

Method: 将GAN训练的阶段切换形式化为序贯假设检验问题。设计两个e-process：(1) 判别器更新时，检验零假设「判别器诱导的经验数据分布与生成器分布之间的分离度低于目标水平」；(2) 生成器更新时（固定判别器），检验反向零假设「该分离度高于刷新水平」。基于训练样本条件，利用新鲜经验指标和隐变量抽取构造条件e值，累积为e-process，在自适应模型更新与数据依赖切换下实现随时有效的I类错误控制。

Result: 在多模态合成分布和图像基准数据集上，该方法在多种广泛使用的GAN目标函数下，匹配或超越了最优固定比率基线的表现。

Conclusion: 基于e-process的自适应训练框架为GAN及其他随机min-max优化问题提供了一种原则性的阶段切换策略，兼具统计严谨性与实际有效性。

Abstract: Modern data science increasingly gives rise to hypothesis-testing problems that are not naturally formulated in terms of parameters within prespecified statistical models. One important example is the dynamic evaluation of optimization algorithms, where decisions must be made during training about whether further updates remain beneficial or the algorithm should switch to a different phase. This issue is particularly relevant in stochastic min-max optimization. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) provide a canonical example, as their training requires repeated decisions about when to switch between discriminator and generator updates, yet existing methods typically rely on fixed update ratios or heuristic criteria. We formulate this switching problem as sequential hypothesis testing and develop an e-process-based adaptive training procedure. During discriminator updates, one e-process tests the null that the discriminator-induced separation between the empirical data distribution and the generator law remains below a target level. During generator updates, with the discriminator fixed, a second e-process tests the reverse null that this separation remains above a refresh level. Conditional on the observed training sample, we prove that fresh empirical indices and latent draws yield conditional e-values that can be accumulated into e-processes, providing anytime-valid Type I error control under adaptive model updates and data-dependent switching. Across multimodal synthetic distributions and image benchmark datasets, the proposed method matches or outperforms the best fixed-ratio baselines under several widely used GAN objectives.

</details>


### [364] [Iterative Erasure Count Is Not an Affine-Invariant Concept Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10566)
*Tingan Jin,Shuhang Dong,Haosong Li,Chung-Hsien Chou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 现有通过迭代擦除探针方向来衡量概念维度的方法，其计数结果（如停止计数、累积擦除秩）在信息保持的可逆重参数化下会发生改变，因此并非内在的概念维度。本文区分了模型定义的总体量（生成维度、充分线性维度、最小保护秩）与过程定义的量，并通过理论构造和实验证明迭代擦除返回的是一个由表示几何和完整测量过程联合决定的过程相对估计量，而非语义维度本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示当前广泛使用的"通过迭代擦除探针方向来测量神经网络表示中概念维度"这一方法论的根本缺陷：擦除计数在可逆重参数化下不稳定，因此不能作为概念维度的内在度量。

Method: 首先在概念上将总体量（生成维度、充分线性维度、最小保护秩）与过程量（停止计数、累积编辑秩）进行区分。然后在高斯总体构造中，通过可逆剪切变换证明：保持预测问题和三个总体量不变的情况下，累积欧氏擦除计数可从一变为二；在全QR过程中累积编辑秩可从二变为环境维度四。进一步证明完整累积度量-QR轨迹在度量、探针、正则化和平局打破规则一致传输时具有仿射等变性。最后通过Adam/QR校准实验和冻结V-JEPA2特征的可控重参数化实验进行验证。

Result: 理论证明：信息保持的可逆剪切变换可改变停止计数和累积编辑秩，但保持所有模型定义的总体量不变。度量-QR轨迹的完全仿射等变性要求度量、探针、正则化和平局打破规则一致传输。实验表明：在已知秩的有限样本Adam/QR校准中，恒等混合在所有20次大样本运行中仅一次更新后停止，而每个测试剪切参数均在所有20次运行中接受至少两次更新；可控重参数化下的V-JEPA2特征保持零秩预测但改变后续欧氏轨迹。

Conclusion: 迭代擦除所返回的是一个过程相对估计量，由表示几何和完整测量过程共同决定，不能单独作为语义维度的度量。概念维度的研究需要区分内在的总体量与依赖于过程的操作量。

Abstract: How many directions does a neural representation use to encode a concept? A common answer repeatedly erases probe directions and reports the stopping count or cumulative removed rank. We show that both quantities can change under an information-preserving invertible reparameterization, so neither is intrinsically a concept dimension. We distinguish model-defined population quantities (generating dimension, sufficient linear dimension, and minimum guarding rank) from procedure-defined quantities such as stopping count and cumulative edit rank. In a population Gaussian construction, an invertible shear preserves the prediction problem and all three quantities, yet changes the cumulative Euclidean erasure count from one to two. The separation holds for Moore--Penrose ordinary least squares and every finite nonnegative ridge weight. For a two-output full-QR procedure matching our motivating video analysis, cumulative edit rank similarly changes from two to the ambient dimension four. Conversely, the complete cumulative metric-QR trajectory is affine-equivariant when its positive-definite metric, probe, regularizer, and tie-breaking are transported consistently; exact covariance is one corollary, not a canonical semantic metric. In a known-rank finite-sample Adam/QR calibration, identity mixing stops after one accepted update in all 20 large-sample runs, whereas each tested shear $a\in\{.5,.75,1,1.25,2\}$ accepts at least two updates in all 20 runs. Controlled reparameterizations of frozen V-JEPA2 features preserve rank-zero predictions yet alter later Euclidean trajectories under practical optimization. These visual contact experiments are stress tests, not estimates of contact dimension. Iterative erasure therefore returns a procedure-relative estimand jointly determined by representation geometry and the full measurement procedure, not a semantic dimension by itself.

</details>


### [365] [Spectral Embeddings of Degree-$α$ Laplacians in Random Dot Product Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10845)
*John Park,Ning Hao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究了网络谱聚类中一类连续的度数归一化谱嵌入方法，在随机点积图模型下建立了行向中心极限定理，揭示了度数归一化如何影响嵌入的总体几何与局部不确定性，并通过贝叶斯误差诊断指出最优归一化取决于网络密度、社区不平衡度及块概率结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有谱聚类方法通常仅使用邻接矩阵或对称拉普拉斯矩阵等少数几种矩阵表示，缺乏对不同度数归一化方式何时及为何能改善聚类性能的统一分布理论理解。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统比较连续归一化族中不同选择的统计性质。

Method: 在随机点积图（RDPG）模型框架下，对一类连续的度数归一化谱嵌入族建立行向中心极限定理；利用极限分布在两社区随机块模型（SBM）中，通过投影高斯贝叶斯误差诊断对不同归一化方式进行定量比较。

Result: 中心极限定理显式刻画了度数归一化如何同时影响总体几何和嵌入节点的局部不确定性。贝叶斯误差比较表明：不存在一致最优的归一化方式；在低密度或社区高度不平衡的场景下，更强的归一化往往更受青睐；最优选择依赖于网络密度、社区不平衡度和块概率结构。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解不同归一化在谱聚类中的表现提供了统一的分布理论基础，揭示了为何及何时替代性归一化能改善聚类性能，对网络数据的谱嵌入实践具有指导意义。

Abstract: Spectral clustering methods for network data are commonly based on a few matrix representations, such as the adjacency matrix and the symmetric Laplacian. We study a continuum of degree-normalized spectral embeddings that includes these commonly used choices as special cases. Under a random dot product graph model, we establish a row-wise central limit theorem for this family of embeddings. The result provides an explicit description of how degree normalization affects both population geometry and the local uncertainty of embedded nodes. We use the limiting distributions to compare different normalizations in two-community stochastic block models through a projected-Gaussian Bayes-error diagnostic. These comparisons show that no single normalization is uniformly preferred. Instead, the favored normalization depends on network density, community imbalance, and block-probability structure. Typically, stronger normalization is favored in lower-density or more imbalanced settings. These results provide a unified distributional understanding of when and why alternative normalizations may improve spectral clustering.

</details>


### [366] [Self-Normalized Inference for Constant-Stepsize Temporal-Difference Learning under Markovian Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10896)
*Min Zeng,Yichen Zhang,Xiaofeng Shao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对固定步长线性TD学习，本文建立了函数中心极限定理，推导了并行Richardson-Romberg外推的联合函数极限，并提出了基于布朗桥自正则化的渐近枢轴置信域方法，无需估计长期协方差。同时研究了随视界递减步长的设计，以实现对投影Bellman解的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定步长TD学习的统计推断面临两大挑战：单条马尔可夫轨迹带来的序列依赖性，以及步长依赖的平稳目标。现有方法需要估计长期协方差或选择带宽/批量大小，本文旨在提供一种无需此类调参的推断框架。

Method: 首先建立固定步长线性TD的函数中心极限定理(FCLT)，其协方差包含随机TD矩阵和平稳迭代误差的乘性分量。然后推导由同一条轨迹驱动的并行Richardson-Romberg外推递归的联合函数极限。利用布朗桥自正则化构造渐近枢轴置信域。还研究了视界索引设计：在每个运行内步长固定，跨更长视界时步长递减，在显式的RR依赖速率窗口下，残余偏移、乘性余项和初始化效应在根号n尺度下可忽略。

Result: 在FrozenLake和Garnet环境上的实验验证了平稳目标覆盖率、RR目标校正效果以及视界索引设计的有限样本表现。对于预设的状态值对比，该方法支持单遍实现，内存不随轨迹长度增长。

Conclusion: 所提出的布朗桥自正则化方法为固定步长线性TD学习提供了计算高效且统计有效的推断框架，避免了传统方法中带宽选择的难题，并通过Richardson-Romberg外推校正了步长引入的偏差。

Abstract: Constant-stepsize temporal-difference (TD) learning is attractive for policy evaluation, but inference from a single Markov trajectory must account for serial dependence and a stepsize-dependent stationary target. For fixed-stepsize linear TD, we establish a functional central limit theorem whose covariance retains the multiplicative component induced by the random TD matrix and the stationary iterate error. We then derive a joint functional limit for parallel Richardson--Romberg (RR) recursions driven by the same trajectory. A Brownian-bridge self-normalizer yields asymptotically pivotal confidence regions for prespecified state-value contrasts without estimating the long-run covariance or selecting a bandwidth or batch length. For such a contrast, the procedure admits a one-pass implementation whose memory does not grow with the trajectory length. At a fixed stepsize, the inferential center is the RR stationary target. We also study horizon-indexed designs in which the stepsize remains constant within each run and decreases across longer horizons. Under an explicit RR-dependent rate window, the residual RR target shift, multiplicative remainder, and initialization effect are negligible at the root-$n$ scale, yielding inference for the projected Bellman solution. Experiments on FrozenLake and Garnet illustrate stationary-target coverage, RR target correction, and the finite-sample behavior of the horizon-indexed design.

</details>


### [367] [Conditional Independence Tests for Constraint-Based Causal Discovery: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11156)
*Pavel Averin,Theodoros Moysiadis,Ioannis Katakis*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文综述了条件独立性（CI）检验方法，聚焦于其在约束型因果发现（如PC和FCI算法）中的应用。文章将主流CI检验方法归纳为六大家族，分析其假设、鲁棒性和在高维混合数据类型场景下的可扩展性，并探讨了检验层面的误差如何传播至图结构层面的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件独立性检验是约束型因果发现算法的核心统计引擎，然而现有方法在面对生物医学领域常见的高维、混合数据类型及有限样本时，存在假设脆弱、功效衰减和鲁棒性不足等问题。论文旨在系统梳理CI检验方法家族，揭示检验级性能如何影响因果图骨架恢复和V结构定向的准确性，并指出开放挑战。

Method: 本文以综述形式，将CI检验方法分为六大家族：偏相关检验、列联表检验、回归检验、最近邻检验、核方法和机器学习检验。针对每个家族，分析了其理论假设（如线性、高斯性）、在条件集增大时的检验功效衰减、I类/II类错误的不对称性，并追踪这些检验级属性如何导致图级别的骨架错误和V结构定向错误。同时比较了R和Python主流库的采用情况。

Result: 综述发现：(1) 各CI检验家族在假设满足时表现良好，但偏离假设时失败模式各异；(2) 条件集维度增长会系统性地削弱检验功效，导致骨架恢复中边的遗漏；(3) 不对称的I类/II类错误会传播为V结构定向中的系统性偏差；(4) 现有方法在处理混合型数据（连续与离散混合）而不经离散化方面仍不成熟；(5) 高维场景下的可扩展性依旧是瓶颈。

Conclusion: CI检验的选择直接影响因果发现的可靠性。未来研究需重点关注：无需离散化的混合型数据CI检验、小样本下的错误控制策略、以及提升CI检验计算可扩展性的方法，以满足生物医学等高维复杂数据的因果发现需求。

Abstract: Conditional Independence (CI) tests are the statistical engine of constraint-based causal discovery: in algorithms such as PC (Peter-Clark) and FCI (Fast Causal Inference), skeleton pruning and key orientations follow directly from CI decisions. This survey reviews CI testing with emphasis on assumptions, robustness, and scalability in high-dimensional and mixed-type settings common in biomedical domains. The survey organizes widely used CI methods into six families: partial-correlation, contingency-table, regression, nearest-neighbor, kernel, and machine-learning-based. Special emphasis is provided on the robustness layers that address the limitations of these families. For each family, the survey examines when CI decisions reflect the data-generating distribution and when they fail. By this, we link test-level properties, including power decay with conditioning set size and asymmetric type I/II error consequences, to graph-level errors in skeleton recovery and v-structure orientation. The survey also compares adoption across major R and Python libraries and summarizes open challenges, including mixed-type CI testing without discretization, small-sample error control, and strategies for improving scalability of CI-testing.

</details>
